# Unfilter digest 04: pregnancy, birth and the first weeks

**Date:** 2026-11-17. **Share:** 1,199 quote records: 502 in [reddit-ur01.jsonl](../voice/reddit-ur01.jsonl), 363 in [reddit-ur18.jsonl](../voice/reddit-ur18.jsonl), and 334 in [reddit-ur19.jsonl](../voice/reddit-ur19.jsonl).

Read [the niche README](../README.md), the current [findings](findings.md), every quote field in the three files, and each ledger’s “What these posts add” section: [ur01](../sources/reddit-reading-ur01.md), [ur18](../sources/reddit-reading-ur18.md), [ur19](../sources/reddit-reading-ur19.md). The full fields below are copied directly from those records. Further IDs preserve support that is repetitive or less specific; some receive their full wording later in Money, Requests or the contrary-evidence sections.

All interpretation is this digest’s reading of the selected quotes. Authors’ medical explanations, treatment effects, risks, legal claims and reported policies are attributed testimony, not facts independently checked here. No network or git was used. Nothing is withheld because of subject. Counts are counts of quote records, never people or prevalence; this is a deliberately selected re-reading of formerly excluded material.

## Changes the final findings most need

- Separate pregnancy count, living children and loss history. Withdraw the assumption that a later pregnancy necessarily means a child at home.
- Give pregnancy loss, decisions about continuation or termination, reproductive coercion and privacy fears their own treatment. The ordinary partner-task and visitor-boundary frames cannot carry all of them.
- Extend care navigation from contacts to conflicting instructions, consent, recall, access, cost and functional capacity. Extend postpartum planning to hospital stays, separation, inability to hold/carry, and inability to sleep during a shift.
- Retain improvement, supportive care, formula or breastfeeding satisfaction, happy later pregnancies, effective reassurance and requests for enjoyment. This selected share cannot tell us their frequency.
- Keep explicit product and service requests separate from peer-experience requests, provider/founder speech and any inference about buying the proposed guide. No record establishes a purchase or price for that guide.

## Evidence and independence

Several IDs are excerpts of one account, not independent corroboration. In particular: [pbu18-0232] through [pbu18-0250] are one detailed birth/recovery narrative; [pbu18-0120] and [pbu18-0121] are the partner’s original account and later improvement update; [pbu01-0249] through [pbu01-0253] concern one family’s twins and uncertain surviving baby; [pbu01-0174] through [pbu01-0177] are one author interpreting an unconfirmed newborn concern. The ledger identifies [pbu19-0339] as another passage of the account in [pbu01-0208] and [pbu01-0209]. Overlap with other shares must be checked by the final synthesizer. The app founder [pbu18-0293] is promotional, and the doula [pbu01-0081], [pbu01-0082] is a provider.

## Patterns

### 1. Pregnancy count, living children and prior losses must be separate selectors.

**Findings location:** §§2, 9, 10 and 16.5. **Relationship:** Contradicts.

**Current conclusion:** “Second-time content must assume she has a child to mind”

**What should change / remain:** Replace that assumption with separate questions about prior pregnancies, living children, losses, gestational ages, neonatal outcomes and the previous birth. A sixth pregnancy can still be a first hoped-for living child; an experienced parent can also be grieving. Do not infer knowledge, childcare needs or reassurance from pregnancy count.

**Evidence:** 6 quote records assigned here for navigation; 6 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

This is my 6th pregnancy and all the others were miscarriages within the first 6w. I know its amazing we got this far and I'm glad to have the extra help.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0230 -->

I conceived them with Letrozole and took progesterone and metformin. In have a bmi of 20 and am considering healthy. I was diagnosed with endometriosis at the end of 2022 and had surgery.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0289 -->

We did blood work and learned that pregnancy wasn’t progressing and I had a D&C which revealed a genetic issue and partially molar. So I’m almost having flashbacks to that, but I am somewhat relieved to have an embryo and heartbeat this time.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0474 -->

Like literally 2, my hcg was 10. At the same ER visit I found out i have moderate fatty liver and stage 2 CKD so ive taken alot of care in changing how I eat and drink. Baby is healthy as of my last check up. My starting weight was 208. Im currently 198 and still losing. Im a little happy that im losing but also wondering if I should be concerned or if other heavier mamas went through this?**
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0180 -->
> I became pregnant again 11 months after my first C-section. During my first pregnancy, I developed preeclampsia at 28 weeks, and my baby passed away two days after delivery.
> 
> It was my first pregnancy, and now I am following up with a new doctor who advised me to take 81 mg aspirin for three months, blood thinner injections, and progesterone/supportive medication.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0351 -->
> I’m pregnant for the 4th time, just 2 months after a graphic 9+ week pregnancy loss. My very first pregnancy ended in an earlier loss, and thankfully I had a rainbow baby between my two losses. I wanted to share my thoughts on the concept of subdued excitement for this new baby. It was a very purposeful and planned pregnancy. In fact, we’d never planned as much as we did for this one. So I feel guilty for not being fully excited for this new baby as I’m still grieving my last one. Selfishly, I feel like I’m guarding my heart against disappointment, rather than being happy for this brand new person, who I still can’t seem to separate from the one I just lost. They are two separate people, and I need to grasp that. When will I feel happy? When I pass 9 weeks? I think I remember feeling better for my rainbow baby again once I passed the timeframe of my very first loss. But I know it’s absurd because there’s no “safe zone.” I’m wrestling with leaving space for multiple feelings at once. We are never all of just one feeling. I’m thankfully pregnant again, and need to take the win while I can. Every life deserves celebration, no matter what. 
> 
> This new baby deserves my excitement.   
> My last baby deserves my grief.   
> My rainbow baby deserves my attention, especially if they’ll have to split it with a new sibling come June. 
<!-- /quote -->

### 2. After loss, ordinary symptoms, symptom relief and appointments can all become reminders that another baby may die.

**Findings location:** §6 (waiting and reassurance), §9 and §17.4. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The previous pregnancy is the yardstick and the previous birth sets the fear.”

**What should change / remain:** Add pregnancy loss itself as an active history, including ectopic pregnancy, missed miscarriage, stillbirth and neonatal death. The feared event can be an ultrasound room, a gestational anniversary, a toilet visit, absent movement or feeling unusually well. A previous living child and a reassuring scan do not necessarily end this experience. Match the requested experience rather than treating every question as an undifferentiated need for reassurance.

**Evidence:** 167 quote records assigned here for navigation; 20 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0030 -->
> i guess i should feel glad those bad symptoms went away, but now im worried that it’s a sign of a miscarriage, whether curent or soon to be. im 5 weeks 4 days today.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0056 -->
> Does anyone have experience conceiving a healthy/viable pregnancy immediately after a miscarriage? Was the experience of TTC/pregnancy different after a loss compared to before, especially for people who already had living children when they experienced loss? Were you still able to enjoy the process of TTC and pregnancy? I have such positive memories of my experiences with my first and with the baby we lost, I'm scared that's gone forever now. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0065 -->
> I am now in the second cycle after the events and we started trying again. I am hopeful but at the same time very very anxious. During the last pregnancy I couldn’t believe it was real - like I couldn’t believe I was actually pregnant, as if something like that could never happen to me. So now I have thoughts like I’m not destined to have a baby, it can’t happen to me, it will all go wrong again. I have all kind of fears that even if I get pregnant and will carry to the term, something will be wrong with the baby. Like it just can’t be that everything will be fine. There are so many things that can go wrong that I don’t believe they won’t happen to me. I even already thought of giving up on the idea altogether. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0131 -->
> I had a baby girl in July 2025, 6 years of infertility, miscarriages, IVF cycles and she arrived safely. She is now 10 months and I as a joke did a test. Mostly because my friend kept saying I was getting emotional. No other side effects. I thought my first pregnancy would teach me that everything is okay, yet I am sitting on edge. I am 4 weeks +3 and I feel like I can’t wait any longer to actually see everything is fine inside? How did you deal with this?
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0153 -->
> I just didn’t expect to worry this much or to feel so sad and anxious in general. It’s such a complete contrast to how I felt during my first pregnancy. The first time I was just excited and happy. This time I feel like I’m just scared.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0205 -->
> so I’m 7w3d today and woke up noticing my breasts aren’t that sore, that might not sound “concerning” but since about 6w they’ve been extremely sore,tender etc but today they’re only a little sore and it’s worrying me as my last pregnancy ended in a miscarriage due to a blighted ovum, my first symptom before I miscarried was loss of breast tenderness I’m just looking for some reassurance I’m still experiencing some other symptoms like moodiness,nausea but this is concerning me and my drs are so so slow I don’t even have an OB yet bc my dr wants to wait another week to refer me bc she doesn’t want to see me until a week after my bloodwork comes in :(
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0239 -->
> I previously had an infant loss at 25 weeks last year due to placenta blood flow. Now I’m pregnant again and I’m worried it might happen again. Has anyone else had this for multiple pregnancy and how did it turn out for your future pregnancies? What did you or your MFM do to prevent this ?
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0324 -->
> I’m 6 weeks and 2 days. I basically feel fine and don’t feel pregnant like at all. This is the most normal and unproblematic pregnancy I’ve had since 2021. I’ve had 6 losses, one being a stillbirth. I can’t shake this feeling that I’ll never have another successful pregnancy. Please help ease my mind or tell me I’m not alone in this. I don’t know if I’ll make it 7 days until my ultrasound without having a full mental breakdown and spiraling. I want to be 12+ weeks already with a cerclage placed so I can sleep a little more at night. I just want this so badly.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0348 -->

But he said he couldn't confirm a viable pregnancy without a heartbeat and that just rocked me. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0349 -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0398 -->

Any insights from people who have been through this? I am so grateful to have several living kiddos to keep me busy, but increasingly am waking up at night and unable to sleep because of fears. <!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0148 -->
> I 25F am on my 4th pregnancy, currently 8 weeks pregnant. This is my 4th pregnancy over the past year and a half. I have had 3 previous miscarriages. This is my first pregnancy with IVF so I have high hopes of it resulting in a live birth. I am high risk due to previous miscarriages and having a blood clotting disorder. Because I am high risk I had my first ultrasound at a little over 6 weeks. I was so excited and felt confident in my pregnancy because of IVF but as soon as I walked into the ultrasound room my mood completely changed. The entire time I was in there I was on edge and felt like I was going to get bad news. Everything was fine, but I still can’t shake the feeling. I am dreading my next appointment this week because I feel like I am going to get bad news and it’s all I can think about. The excitement has completely faded. Has anyone else felt like this after loss or losses? How did you get over the fear?
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0181 -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0316 -->
> I never ever thought I would make a post like this, but here we go.   
> I’m looking for other moms that have had positive experiences with a sch.

I was scared, but happy baby was okay. Went to the ob next day, it had shrank to 1.9cm. Awesome. Put on pelvic floor rest and told not to worry. Fast forward one week and a day so 10 weeks. ### 3. A loss has its own physical recovery, practical dismantling, grief and decisions about trying again.

**Findings location:** New loss/recovery subsection within §§2, 6 and 9; qualify §10. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Make the first two weeks a full section: feeding, sleep, recovery supplies, the partner's shifts, visitors.”

**What should change / remain:** Include the possibility that a pregnancy ends without a baby coming home: continuing bleeding, D&C or other treatment, cycle uncertainty, waiting at work, putting away purchases and resetting shared apps. Some want to try again immediately; some stop trying for their mental health; some describe a manageable early loss. None is interchangeable with a newborn-at-home plan.

**Evidence:** 41 quote records assigned here for navigation; 22 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0031 -->
> And it happened. I found out I was pregnant this week and now I am not anymore, it was a chemical pregnancy. I didn't think it would be so hard on me, but it is. I am so in pain. I was already imagining my two kids playing together... reimagining the rooms... 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0179 -->
> I'm 9 weeks pp from my stillbirth. I had my first period start at 5 weeks postpartum and it ran until 7 weeks postpartum. I had gone down to lochia alba before that so I know for sure it wasn't lochia. It was super heavy the first week and then tapered off like normal, but then I had two more weeks of very light bleeding/spotting that finally seems to have stopped today. I've been checking for ovulation with LH strips every day for the past week and a half and they're not really changing at all. I'm not having any of my normal signs of ovulation (no EWCM, my boobs don't hurt, and I'm not getting more breakouts like I usually do post ovulation) but I'm wondering if I just missed it or if it happened while I was still bleeding. We're not trying, in fact we're actively avoiding it as I had a c-section and have to heal for a few more months. In the past we've very successfully avoided pregnancy by tracking my ovulation but I also had a predictable cycle. This is uncharted territory for me and I'm wondering if anyone else has been in a similar situation. I know that my hormones still haven't truly settled and that it will likely be wonky for a while.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0185 -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0186 -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0007 -->
> I lost my second baby on Monday after 16 weeks. Heartbroken is an understatement. We have one 14 month old boy and we were so looking forward to a sibling for him. After a viral infection I went for a scan and it showed the worst - no fluid around baby and no heart beat.
> 
> I’m already 41. Do I try again? I feel guilty for already thinking about trying again as I want to mourn my baby boy, except time isn’t on my side. Feeling sad empty and depressed. I need something to look forward to, to help me with my grief.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0008 -->
> On the 20th week mark, my brain woke me up with a panic attack. I dream of the surgery room/table often.
> 
> I'm far from jealous but I just...I feel bitter...when I say a baby or a pregnant mom ...im trying to be the bigger kinder person and I only wish peace and love and support to every woman out there but gosh it hurts so bad.
> 
> My sister in law just gave birth and I called her husband and everything, but I gushed into tears afterwards, my mother in law called me saying "hey your turn will come and you'll have a healthy pregnancy " and she means well and she's supportive but her words kill me..
>

<!-- quote:pbu18-0048 -->
> At 24 weeks, I started to get really sick and by 25 weeks I had HELLP Syndrome with multiple acute organ injuries. We were told the baby had a 10% chance to live but he made it overnight and then day I told my husband we need to stay possible that anything is possible. By end of week 1, he was thriving then they want to do a pic line and that the chances of infection was less than 1%. By the end of the week 2, it was a U turn, he had sepsis. Att 2am we got the worse call to come down to the NICU immediately, he was crashing. 
> 
> Our baby passed away as soon as we arrived to the NICU. And the most unbearable pain anyone could is what I was feeling. 
> 
> 10 months later, we got pregnant again unplanned and I miscarried at 5 weeks then 11 more times (over 3 years) everytime we tried we would get pregnant but I would have miscarry/chemical pregnancy before 5 wks 1 day. 2 of those times I had an ectopic pregnancy and lost one of my fallopian tubes. 
> 
> A million test later was classified as not infertile as we would get pregnant each time and that my scar tissue in my uterus should have not been an issue as my fertility doctor said women with way more fibroids or other obstruction then finally deemed unknown etiology. For a last resort they were going to remove all the scar tissue by HSG but then I would have to repeat another 1 or 2 more HSGs before trying.  Exhausted with this journey, I did a hard stop and we avoided trying to get pregnant for the next year. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0049 -->

The Dr stayed quiet and frustrated saying, “ugh stop popping up” some text with the measurement was popping up then with a straight face with zero emotional,  “There isn’t a heart beat, I can’t find one.” Then he keeps having issues with the US and pop up. Then I asked if he was 100%. Then he said he was going to get the head US technician. And it was like a F-ing movie the other person comes in starts look around and silent. Then I see the measurements at 8wk 0 days then my heart sank. He said, “I can’t find the heartbeat via US, vascular or Doppler, I’m sorry.” “Get dressed and I will give you guys some privacy.”

### 4. Parents can be grieving one baby, carrying another and caring for a living child at the same time.

**Findings location:** §§2, 6 and 9; proposed grief-during-pregnancy subsection. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Another pregnancy is a different capacity and history.”

**What should change / remain:** Make room for simultaneous loss and continuation, especially a demised twin, a surviving baby with uncertain prognosis, and the living child who needs an explanation. Buying, naming, reading to the baby and celebrating can involve anticipatory grief rather than ordinary procrastination. Do not turn the surviving baby into a replacement for the one who died.

**Evidence:** 10 quote records assigned here for navigation; 10 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0206 -->
> Now that I'm pregnant again, we are very hesitant to do much in the way of preparation because of our fears. But, we have started to talk a bit about names.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0207 -->
> Also, we weren't setting out to find a way to specifically honor her in the next child's name, but I know people will ask if we named the boy after our girl. Which might just get annoying, but obviously we could just say no. But then... is it bad that we just happened upon an M name and AREN'T trying to honor our daughter in this way? We do honor her in various other ways and I don't know that I want to saddle our boy with the weight of an honor name for his sister who came before him. But am I thinking of this wrong? 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0249 -->
> We press on. We are offered amniocentesis to check for T21 in our surviving baby, but told it is probably unnecessary as the demised twin was showing all the signs (elevated NT specifically) and the other one shows none. The twins were fraternal so had their own DNA. We couldn't stomach the risk of miscarriage, however small, just to know, so we declined. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0250 -->
> We decide based on the fetal cardiology appointment no termination because if there is a good chance at life we will take it. One half of our family supports us, one half raises an eyebrow. I understand that is an extremely personal decision especially with T21, but having the judgement and the “have you really thought about this” type questions was hard.   
> (For added context I am a nurse of almost 10 years and worked in pediatrics for 7. Also worked in the NICU for a time. I am no stranger to special needs or medically fragile children). 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0251 -->
> I am set to be induced at 37 weeks. I am 32 weeks now. I go for testing two times a week. My demised twin only disappeared from ultrasounds less than a month ago after I slowly watched him flatten and evaporate. Every appointment is tense and scary wondering if something has changed for the worse. The cholestasis is horrific at times. We have one more appointment with fetal cardiology where they will tell us their final opinion before he is born. There is emphasis on the fact it is a spectrum of things- not necessarily fixable and not necessarily not fixable, it will be a judgment call based on how he looks and how his heart is after birth. This is such a huge difference to what we were told at the first fetal cardiology appointment. Regardless of any test results, he will be taken away from me after birth to the childrens hospital which is something I feel very emotional about. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0252 -->
> I am scared to buy things for him. I have declined baby showers because I am in a tough place of what if he doesnt survive. I read to my daughter every night when I was pregnant and intended to do so with this baby, but I can't bring myself to get more attached than I already am. The anticipatory grief is intense. My husband is more optimistic than I am, but he has at times broken down which is so hard to see because there's nothing I can say or do to fix what he is feeling. I know he is trying to be strong for me and I appreciate it. Having to explain to my daughter that her little brother she was ecstatic about is very sick and may not be coming home is hard. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0253 -->
> I also work full time which I know many of us do, but it's just another layer to the exhaustion and the “when is your baby due omg you must be so excited.”

<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0275 -->
> I’m 32, FTM. I suffer from Stage 4 Endometriosis and Adenomyosis and was diagnosed with Primary Infertility. After that I was put on Orilissa Suppression for 3 mnths and did my embryo transfer.  
> I got pregnant with DIDI twins and we were extremely elated. Everything was fine till week 15.   
> At our 20 week Anatomy scan in June 2026, they found Twin A to be severely growth restricted <3 percentile which Twin B was 72nd percentile.   
> We were referred to MFM and the next scan showed Twin B dropping to <1 percentile at 21w5d.   
> We had another scan at 24W to determine viability and that’s when we heard what we never expected to hear.   
> Twin A had passed away in the womb. Twin B was growing well.   
> Ever since, it’s been so frustrating and negative for that I am slowing losing hope, my mind and have lost all happiness even though Twin B is low risk according to doctors and will continue to term since they were didi and didn’t share a placenta. 
>

My MFM suggested that I see a mental health provider but I think there’s so much to talk about that I’d rather not talk about anything.
>

### 5. Some readers are deciding whether to continue a pregnancy, terminate it, choose adoption or parent without the relationship.

**Findings location:** New pregnancy-decision subsection in §§1, 6, 9 and 10. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The decision and care experience need their own treatment.”

**What should change / remain:** Expand “decision” beyond induction, pain relief and mode of birth. Include genuine uncertainty, a settled termination decision with overwhelming feelings, attachment alongside a wish to end pregnancy, interrupted abortion attempts, adoption, and continuation without a partner. Resources, previous loss, religion, fertility fears, earlier abortion experiences and existing children can pull in different directions. Delays in access and intolerable symptoms are part of the experience. These are not automatically requests for a birth-preparation product.

**Evidence:** 93 quote records assigned here for navigation; 36 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

> 
> It’s just a lot. I know things could work themselves out but the idea of doing this alone is scary with so many unknowns. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0170 -->
> My husband and I (25yo) are financially stable just the two of us, but it will be difficult to afford the baby and I’m really unsure if my job will allow maternity leave. This terrifies me as we need my job in order to stay afloat. I have been in mental agony trying to decide what to do. Ultimately I’ve decided that I wouldn’t be able to forgive myself if I terminated the pregnancy (just knowing myself) and maybe somehow someway this baby is meant to be. My husband insisted I made the decision as it’s my body and he just wants me to be happy. I of course took his feelings into account and I know he is just as if not more terrified as me but we’ve been through so much together in our 10 years and we’ve always made it out on top. We’re going to be frugal and we’re going to make this work. I haven’t told my mom yet and for some reason am so scared to. My mom is my absolute best friend, is so supportive, and had me when she was 20. I feel bad that I haven’t told her yet but I’m just afraid that she might be disappointed. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0193 -->
> \- A baby doesn’t fix a relationship.  
> \- We’re not together, and after an entire year apart I’ve still been unsure if I even want to be with him.  
> \-Him suddenly “locking in” after finding out about the pregnancy is honestly a turnoff. When I’ve been asking for that energy 4 years ago.   
> \- I genuinely cannot stand his older sister.  
> \- His mom drives me crazy sometimes(doesn’t know boundaries/intrusive), although she’d probably be the most involved grandma ever.  
> \- I always pictured having kids around 32-35, not 27.  
> \- I wanted to be married first.  
> \- I imagined being in an even more stable financial position before becoming a mom.  
> \- Traveling becomes a lot harder.  
> \- Having a child and a dog adds permanent responsibilities.  
> \- I’m afraid of not being as dateable in the future.  
> \- I’m scared of potential disabilities or medical challenges and whether I’d be equipped to handle them.  
> \- I’m scared of ending up carrying most of the parenting load.
> 
> That last one comes from experience. When we lived together, I was the one doing most of the household management. I walked the dogs more, cleaned more, cooked more, and we split bills 50/50. Even when I asked for help, it often felt like I had to manage everything. I don’t want that dynamic for the rest of my life.
> 
> The thing is, I am capable of raising a child. I have a good career, own a small business, live on my own, and pay all my bills. This isn’t really about whether I *can* do it.
> 
> It’s more that I pictured my first pregnancy happening under very different circumstances older, more financially secure, more mentally prepared, married, and with someone I felt completely confident building a life with.  
> I’ve also been doing a lot of research, and next Thursday I’ll be 10 weeks. I’ll likely hear the heartbeat and see the embryo. Knowing that the baby’s organs and features are already developing is making this decision feel even heavier from a moral standpoint.
> 
> I’m genuinely torn and don’t know what to do.
> 
> For anyone who has been in a similar situation whether you chose to continue the pregnancy or not what factors helped you make your decision, and do you have any regrets? 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0263 -->
> My son will be 2 next month, I accidentally got pregnant again and I’m now 9 weeks pregnant. I’m still with my son’s dad but it’s been a long, unhealthy relationship I have been needing/wanting to leave for a long time. It’s just hard, iykyk. I know I sound like an idiot for getting pregnant again because I am, we just had a drunken weekend that put me here. I wasn’t gonna keep it or tell him but he found out and it changed everything. He is a great dad and was excited, I leaned to my mom for advice and she was excited about the news, he told his family who was ecstatic. It messed with my head a lot. I’ve always wanted multiple kids and for my son to have a sibling, especially this close in age. 
> 
> This part is gonna sound messed up. I felt pressure to not telling anyone I didn’t know if I wanted it. I ended up getting abortion pills to terminate at home and essentially fake a miscarriage. I inserted them and regretted, taking them out 30ish minutes later. I bled and passed clots, I went to the er a couple days later and confirmed my pregnancy was continuing. I did feel happiness and relief. This was at 6 weeks/4 days. 
> 
> Here I am at 9 weeks scared. I don’t want to give up the only years of my 20s I have left. I’m 25 I’ve wasted 5 years with a man who’s really only made my life harder. I’ve been planning to leave and this happens. My heart breaks for myself for being so dumb and wasting my life and potential. We are broke, my credit has gone down, I’ve been held back by dare I say…. A bum. I know I’m an idiot. I have a Career path planned but I fear I’m going to ruin it by having another baby. Part of me thinks I can do it but I’d have to leave him or I’d hate myself for being weak and just resent him. So I’d be pregnant, working, planning my life single and obviously coparenting. But part of me wants to make a decision for myself for once, walk away from the relationship unpregnant and start my independent journey as a young woman. The problem is I feel a connection to my belly, when I went to the er, they did an ultrasound and I saw the heartbeat, that fucked me up. I look at my son and feel so fucking selfish for wanted to terminate this one. I’m terrified I’ll regret it. But Idk what I’ll regret more, keeping it, or choosing me. Please help me. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0274 -->
> I've sadly been met with extreme coercion from my mom, who has been determined that I get an abortion from day 1 and quite cruel in making this known. That I'd be ruining everyone's life and my own if I went through this as a single mother. That I'd be a terrible parent, that I haven't lived enough yet at 35 to be trapped with a baby, that I'm abandoning her. This severe lack of emotional support from my own mother was so hurtful and caused a lot of trauma. I wish I had been given the support to make a choice for myself as an adult, and not be infantilized as a teen mom (which wouldn't be right either, of course, but I'm certainly not 16!) She has stated that if I wait a few years and have a planned pregnancy through IVF, it will be easier for her to be there to support me. She just doesn't like \*this\* pregnancy because it is unplanned, she manages a lot of other family responsibilities (all legitimate), and it hasn't given her the time to mentally prepare.
> 
> Even though I stand to inherit money one day, I'm not currently in a financial situation where I'd be able to deal with this all on my own, and it doesn't seem like I'd have much hands-on help from my family right now. I'm also extremely career-oriented (since that's one of the only parts of my life I \*have\* been able to somewhat control, unlike relationships), so I certainly would be committed to staying at my job and would have to put a baby in daycare very early, which is of course prohibitively expensive. And also being in the US, I have no maternity leave and would have to use short-term disability or FMLA for the short time I'd be able to stay home with the baby. Just so many roadblocks that make any other decision feel impossible & frankly irresponsible. All this has prompted me to schedule a termination, because logically, I don't see any other way.
> 
> I went to my initial pre-abortion appointment and couldn't stop crying, and had to cancel the original planned procedure a few weeks ago. There's been so many ups and downs over the past few months - some days I feel OK in my decision, other days I cannot stop sobbing at the thought of ending my pregnancy. Even though the procedure is now rescheduled, I have a hard time imagining myself actually walking into a hospital and willingly terminating. I feel so much grief for something that hasn't even happened yet - I desperately wish I were in a situation where it \*could\* all work out and feel resentful of my life circumstances that are preventing me from having this, especially after I've worked so hard in my career and education.
> 
> Ideally, yes, I would have waited another few years to go through the IVF process to be better prepared, and not have a potentially complicated/clandestine paternity situation. But part of me can't help feeling like I was meant to have \*this\* child, which seems to exist against all odds. And of course, all the big what-ifs. Who would this child become? What would life look like in 1, 10, 20 years? What if I can't have another child, and this is my only chance? Will I regret not trying harder to make it work, even though it would be impossibly difficult? I guess it's the hormones, but I already feel so connected to this baby.
> 
> I'm just hanging onto the stories throughout this sub where people share that they're ultimately grateful for their past abortions, which allowed them to bring their living children into the world when they were more ready. I just hope that can be me too.
> 
> I just don't know what to do. I just feel like I'm going to regret termination for the rest of my life, but I'm terrified to enter into a life of being a single mom without any support at this time. 
> 
> Sometimes I also can't quite tell if this is \*actually\* what I think or want, or if all the pregnancy hormones are clouding my judgment. 
> 
> Apologies for the long post, but I appreciate anyone's thoughts on similar experiences, making the most difficult decision of our lives, and navigating abortion grief. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0330 -->
> After much back and forth, after all the mind changing, after many cancelled/missed abortion appointments, I am at the point (18 weeks) where I’ve come to term with the fact that I’m keeping my baby. I’m STILL unsure and half the time feel regretful but I’ve accepted that I’m doing this. The father has made this experience very unhappy for me. We’re always up and down, on and off, and I finally realized we were never compatible. One of the biggest reasons I didn’t want to do this was because I decided I don’t want anything to do with this man ever again, a clean break. I know he will still be involved in his child’s life whether we’re together or not, I know the misery he’s bringing me now is only the beginning and this was a huge reason for my uncertainty. But we’re here now, no turning back and I guess I’m doing this. I’m rarely excited, afraid to be a “single” mom. Disappointed the family unit future I pictured for myself is not happening. Slightly embarrassed that I let a life I never saw for myself be my reality and afraid this new life style is one that I will accept but may not bring me happiness but these are things I will have no choice but to accept in the long run. These are my thoughts now as a 31 year old with no children. I do hope that the birth of this child brings about a whole new happiness and meaning for myself but I’m in my grief stage right now. ### 6. A partner or relative may control pregnancy, sex, money, movement, treatment or custody rather than fail to help.

**Findings location:** §5, §6, §9 and §17.7; revise the limits of §10’s partner prescription. **Relationship:** Contradicts.

**Current conclusion:** “The partner piece: short, task-based, audio or video, framed as his job”

**What should change / remain:** That prescription is inadequate for these accounts. Retain it for a willing support person, and add coercion as a different problem: pressure both to continue and to terminate, confinement until an abortion window closes, rape, physical violence, strangulation, financial control, custody threats and refusal of recommended testing. A stable income, marriage, a wanted baby or a father’s attachment to the baby do not establish maternal safety. This selected share cannot establish how common any of this is.

**Evidence:** 64 quote records assigned here for navigation; 64 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

He only does the chores he enjoys. I still do 80% of the housework. He insists he's not depressed and loves it here but he does not do shit. We're also no longer intimate because I'm in bed exhausted at 9 pm and he's up all night gaming.
> 
> 
> 
> Work has been absolute hell. I have so much more work now that I'm pregnant because everyone knows I'm leaving soon. I am still expected to pull 12+ hour days like nothing's wrong. Can barely make it out of bed most days and can barely do my job anymore. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0015 -->
> The thing is, we didn’t really even talk about trying for a baby? It was more unspoken? I don’t know. We got married, and he just started finishing inside me. After the first couple times, I told him it made me feel uncomfortable, but I didn’t fully know why.
> 
> So now I’m just… not thrilled. I feel more upset than excited. Honestly, I feel like my life is suddenly over or completely changed, and I hate that I feel this much resentment toward the whole thing, but I do. Does that change? I want to get over it and pivot and accept it, but right now I just feel bummed and overwhelmed. Am I a horrible person? 
> 
> It also doesn’t help that he’s completely overjoyed and already talking about getting a house, changing my diet, establishing routines, all healthy and responsible things. But I don’t know. I’m just not happy.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0024 -->
> Last week, on the exact day we were flying and executing a massive international relocation, my husband was secretly exporting and saving links to highly provocative women's profiles into a private, hidden folder. > When I confronted him with the proof, he gave a brief apology but immediately turned it around on me. He completely blamed our lack of intimacy over the past year—essentially weaponizing my pregnancy and postpartum recovery to excuse his actions as just "handling his personal needs."
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0025 -->
> Suddenly, the conversation is no longer about his secret actions or breach of trust—it’s entirely about how I am supposedly breaking him. I feel like the trust is permanently shattered and I want to take my baby and go back home to my family, but I am financially dependent on him right now and feel trapped.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0036 -->
> Title may seem misleading, i’m really grateful to be a mother soon and to have a baby, but my husbands and I’s relationship is horrible.
>

>

> 
> So here I am, 2 hours before going in and we’re sitting in silence ignoring eachother and I’ve never felt more alone, empty and broken in my life— I feel deep down i’ve made a horrible mistake, but even saying that makes me feel so guilty.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0042 -->
> The issue is I really don’t want this baby, I feel like I was slightly pressured into keeping it and when I did book a termination my partner talked me out of it and practically had me on house arrest until the window allowing for abortion closed… (he booked time off work when ever I did, dropped me off at work and picked me up etc… there was no way I would have been able to access an abortion) 
> 
> Now I know that the facts indicate that I am pregnant, but at the same time it doesn’t quite feel real? It feels more like I have cancer and that instead of being treated, I’m being encouraged to allow the cancer to kill me? I don’t know how to explain it. 
> 
> I also really can’t imagine having a baby, I can’t imagine life with one. Usually if I can’t imagine something it means that it’s not going to happen, I think this could be my body’s way of telling me that I’m going to die or that the baby will die. I’m coming to terms that this is going to be the case, and I don’t really mind.
> 
> I don’t know if this is normal or not, I’m being supported for my prenatal depression by the perinatal mental health team, but I’m not sure I want to talk to them about this. They keep telling me that they can help after, with bonding… how do I explain to them that this isn’t necessary? 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0082 -->
> I'm pregnant and also have an 8 year old. My relationship is breaking down and I'm terrified. He's also my carer and I depend on him for transport as there's no public transport or taxis where I live. I've also tried moving somewhere more accessible but the council won't help and I can't afford private. I rely on him to take me to appointments and my child to school. I need a c section too so will need help then. I have no family whatsoever, or real friends who care. He also said if we break us he's taking the baby, which I'm also scared of as his family, who he hasn't spoken to for years, are addicts and not nice people, and he will probably end up living with them if we did break up, when I do not want my baby around them. I feel like I've failed my baby already for my poor choices in life. My older child is with someone else, who I get on with, but her dad is unavailable to take her to school due to work, and also has a very small family who are also unavailable. School can't help either. I physically cannot walk far at all. I had a pregnancy loss last year so this baby is an absolute blessing and was unexpected, but I am over the moon and can feel his little kicks already. But I feel like a failure as I'm having to fake a relationship with this man just to make sure I and the kids are okay, as I can't do it alone. I feel like I don't deserve to be a mum. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0099 -->
> My husband cheated on me while I was 6 months pregnant. He cheated with a woman who has 2 children of her own. While I was carrying his baby and looking after our 1 year old, he was building a new life behind my back. 
> 
> He became abusive. I had him arrested for domestic abuse, and now he’s turned everything around on me. He’s telling people I’m the abusive one, making me look like some bitter, scorned woman instead of someone whose whole world has just been destroyed.
> 
> I miscarried our baby at 6 months pregnant. I went through that pain while dealing with betrayal, trauma, police, accusations, and trying to survive for my little boy. Now it’s just me and my 1 year old.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0100 -->
> I can’t even put him on child support because he doesn’t have a legitimate job or income to trace. I have no money, no partner, no support, and every day feels like I’m carrying the weight of the world alone.
> 
> I genuinely can’t understand how someone can walk away from their own child so easily, especially after everything we’ve been through. I keep asking myself what I did to deserve this.
>

<!-- quote:pbu18-0112 -->

It stemmed from a lot of different issues he had with me but the most notable is that I refused to send him “pictures of my body” (yes those were the words he chose) during my pregnancy. He continuously asked in a way that made me very uncomfortable, and I also just felt self conscious about the way that I looked, and I didn’t even take very many pictures when I was pregnant (something that I now regret a lot.)

<!-- quote:pbu18-0159 -->

But he has also made it clear he does not want to marry me. When I said I wanted to step back from intimacy because I’m trying to stop falling into sexual sin, he said he was not okay with that. His reasoning was that my sexual needs will be higher during pregnancy and he wants to be the one to meet them.
> 
> He has also said at one point that I should abort — and I’ll be honest, there was a moment I almost wanted him to say it so I’d have someone to blame it on. He said it. But when I wavered and said maybe I’d keep it, he flipped and started making plans and being supportive.
> 
> I cannot trust this man. I cannot build a life with him. I do not want to marry him. And I do not want my child to grow up to be like him.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0165 -->
> At the end of March I tested positive as a carrier for a recessive autoimmune genetic condition, and my OB strongly recommended that the baby’s father undergo genetic testing through maternal fetal medicine and meet with a geneticist so we could assess the baby’s risk and create a medical plan. He initially agreed, but later outright refused the testing, saying it would not change the outcome when we met in person to talk about things on April 2nd (last month). This was devastating to me because I felt he was refusing an important medical responsibility and placing our child’s health at risk as I may have to undergo a amniocentesis now that he refused testing.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0166 -->
> Now I feel conflicted. Part of me feels he deserves to be there because he is the father, but another part of me feels deeply betrayed, abandoned, and emotionally unsafe. I worry seeing him will set me back in my healing. He attended my 12 week scan and we were very pleasant (had only been broken up for a week or two back then) and we were laughing and happy. Immediately after the scan went right back to ghosting me which caused me a lot of emotional pain. 
> 
> I had pretty much accepted he wasn’t coming and my OB last week told me it’s important to bring my mom or dad with me to the scan as it is a major milestone (I was planning on going alone as I am a nurse and don’t really get intimated by hospital settings). My mom is Florida moving my brother into his new house so my dad agreed to go with me. My dad is absolutely disgusted by my ex and his family (they also have not checked in once or reached out to me during my pregnancy) and I don’t think it would go well to have both of them there for the scan. I just feel like seeing him will set me back emotionally and I know he will just go no contact once the scan is over and appear whenever he wants since it’s convienant to him. He doesn’t know I’m planning to bring my dad. I also have major anxiety around texting/ reaching out to him as throughout the whole first month after we broke up he would ignore me and I would cry for hours with horrible anxiety and panic. 
> 
> I’m in a better place now and have accepted we will not be together. I truly do not want him to come tomorrow as I have made significant progress and don’t want to risk taking 10 steps back and go back to crying and being constantly anxious. I know it’s unhealthy for the baby and really do not want to open that can of worms. The only thing I am worried about is if he takes it personally as if I am now allowing him to come out of spite or resentment and will try to paint me as not including him later once the baby is born  if he wants custody. I plan on not filing for child support as I am not putting his name on the birth certificate. I of course am not keeping the baby away from him but I really worry about 50/50 custody during the first year as he will be breastfeeding. I am also a pediatric/ NICU nurse and have extensive knowledge on childcare whereas my ex has none and won’t have me there to help him.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0179 -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0183 -->

I have no job, no friends, all I have is him and my cat. I hate my life right now. How do I turn this into a happy thing when it’s not something I want. I wanted to wait and enjoy being pregnant. But I wanted it in a couple years. Not this soon.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0189 -->

I will go for the abortion because it is more logical with the situation but I dont feel happy or relieved by that choice, also I do not want to tell my future child “your dad never wanted nothing to do with you”.

I feel so trapped because I quit my job and he pays all my bills. <!-- quote:pbu18-0281 -->
> My boyfriend kept telling me that I needed to get an abortion and I kept telling him that I need time to decide because this would be a decision that would stay with me forever. I talked to my mom and she said that I need to do what I want to do because it’s my body so I went to a woman’s clinic that wasn’t super opinionated. I looked into my options and decided I was going to carry on with my pregnancy. I allowed my boyfriend to see my ultrasound and he refused to talk to me the entire day. The next day my boyfriend was pissed and told me that I HAVE to get an abortion. For days I laid in my bed, sobbing because I felt like I was being pressured into a decision I didn’t want to make. He was super insensitive towards me and would ask “so when are you going to do the dishes?” While I’m curled up on the bed crying. I felt so betrayed. The moment I stood up to him was when he told me that I’m “baby trapping him”. (For reference I wasn’t on birth control and I made that very clear to him). I told him that I’m keeping the baby no matter what and that it’s his decision if he wants to be in our kids life. Now he’s telling EVERYONE that I’m pregnant and how happy he is. I told him I don’t feel comfortable telling anyone before 12 weeks but he decided to not listen. All the people he had told reached out to me and congratulated me but I feel bittersweet about it. I’m happy he changed his mind but I still feel upset. My baby’s first ultrasound was supposed to be an amazing moment but he ruined it by trying to bully me into an abortion. I love that his family/friends are excited but they don’t see how he treated me. Idk maybe I’m over reacting but I’ve been holding this in for weeks.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0290 -->
> So I 23f was assaulted on a college campus in Texas by who I thought was a friend. That said person had threatened me with a knife and the whole experience was genuinely terrible. I did what was forced of me and I left and got a plan b. Unfortunately it did not work and I became pregnant. I was devastated because I have always wanted to become a mother just not like this. Recently in Texas abortion pills, traveling for abortions, and even threats of abortions have became illegal. I had to choose between my autonomy and getting justice against my assaulter. I tried to choose autonomy but I couldn’t do it. The week I was gonna go to get the procedure a woman was arrested while traveling for an out of state abortion and abortion pills had became extremely illegal. I felt trapped. It was too late to come out lab out the assault legally and I had to carry my child to term. At this point I became attached to my baby and I decided to raise her on my own. Despite all of the pain and suffering I had endured that decision to keep her was truly my own. I hold no resentment against her. In her own way she is the blessing, she is the light that came from all this darkness, she is with me.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0311 -->
> He is also extremely stressed himself, working three jobs at the same time, and I try to keep everything at home under control as much as I possibly can.  
> Sleep, however, is a very sensitive issue for me. I’ve struggled with serious sleep problems in the past. During this pregnancy, I’ve actually been falling asleep really well, but if someone wakes me up after I’ve fallen asleep, I usually can’t get back to sleep until morning — and it genuinely makes me angry.
> 
> Tonight, the same thing happened again, for the second time within a fairly short period.  I also explained the situation last time. I was already sleeping peacefully when my partner woke me up and started talking to me about something. Then he happily rolled over and went back to sleep, while I immediately realized that my sleep was completely gone, even though I was absolutely exhausted.
> 
> I got angry because all I could think was, “Again? What gives you the right?” I threw my pillow around, muttered something to myself, and went into another room. After a while, he followed me, and that’s when everything turned into a complete disaster.
> 
> He screamed at me because now he couldn’t sleep either but he has to go to work. He threw me outside while I was naked (although after some time he did let me back in). I am always afraid that he will literally punch me one day. He repeated the same old things about how useless I am and how I’ve achieved nothing in my life. For context, I have a master’s degree with honors and a stable job in the energy sector — I just earn a fairly ordinary middle-class corporate salary.
> 
> He also told me that everything has gone badly for him professionally because we are together. There is absolutely no connection or logic behind that. I’ve even changed aspects of my appearance and the way I dress because he wanted me to.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0312 -->
> Before I met him, I was perfectly capable of supporting myself and managing my own life. Now I contribute to maintaining both his house and apartment. Of course, contributing is completely normal because I live there too, but maintaining two homes is simply too much on my salary, especially when one of them needed to be completely renovated. I didn’t choose this lifestyle — I chose him. And yet he holds it against me that I’m not financially capable of contributing enough to maintain that lifestyle.
> 
> I cook, I clean, I plan everything, I practically act as his personal assistant, and yet most of the time I feel like a doormat.
> 
> And despite all of this, I desperately want our child to have parents who are together. I know that sometimes separating is healthier for everyone involved, especially for a child. But I constantly have this feeling that if we could just get rid of the financial stress and make our lives more stable, maybe we wouldn’t fight so much. And on the good days, it genuinely feels like everything could be wonderful.
> 
> But then something happens again…  
> And I’m tired of constantly having to swallow everything and being made to feel terrible about myself.
> 
> More than anything, though, I’m worried about our unborn baby. How does all this stress and fighting affect him? Sometimes I actually feel ashamed when I think about what his parents are doing while he’s right there inside me. More than anything, I just need to know that my baby is okay.
> 
> Right now, I don’t even want advice telling me to “just leave him.” I don’t have the strength for that. I’ve invested so much into both of our homes, everything for the baby, and our life together.  
> I think I just need some support. I don’t really believe in self-pity, but right now I genuinely feel like a complete doormat. 😔

### 7. Disclosure can bring support, unwanted exposure, pressure or fear of losing the right to parent.

**Findings location:** §§5–6, §9 and §17.2’s care-navigation page. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The "who to ask" page earns its place.”

**What should change / remain:** Account for the cost of asking: fear of CPS/social-care involvement, compulsory sharing of medical history, undisclosed drug testing, hospitalization after mentioning suicidal thoughts and paternity disclosure after rape. Separate reported fears and experiences from verified policy. Announcement timing can protect privacy after loss, preserve work functioning or avoid repeated emotional labor; it is not simply a celebration milestone.

**Evidence:** 19 quote records assigned here for navigation; 19 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

### 8. A wanted pregnancy can coexist with panic, repulsion, resentment, detachment, rage or suicidal thoughts.

**Findings location:** §§1–3, §6 (fear, shame and identity) and §9. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Focus on her body and life, not the baby's fruit size”

**What should change / remain:** Give the prenatal emotional experience its own account rather than assuming excitement until postpartum. Symptoms can be physically mild while mental capacity is nearly absent. Several authors feel guilty about not loving the pregnancy, or anxious that anxiety itself will harm the baby. Delayed connection, ambivalence and the distress of an unwanted situation should not be collapsed into one diagnosis. Include explicit self-harm and suicidal language as written.

**Evidence:** 55 quote records assigned here for navigation; 30 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0092 -->
> I am currently in my first trimester. Before getting pregnant, I had a history of severe anxiety, but I was on Fluoxetine (Prozac) and was doing incredibly well. To conceive, I tapered off the medication under medical supervision and remained stable and perfectly fine for over 90 days. We wanted this baby; my fiancé and I made plans, and everything felt warm and right in my mind.    
> The shock hit the exact second I saw the positive test. It felt like a fuse blew in my brain. Instantly, a violent, paralyzing panic triggered, and it hasn't stopped since.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0093 -->
> The biggest issue—the thing that is completely breaking me mentally and making me feel like an absolute monster—is that instead of joy, any discussion or thought about the baby triggers physical repulsion, emotional nausea, and extreme anxiety. When I try to look at the future, I see only a cold, pitch-black screen. I am crying uncontrollably out of guilt toward my family and the baby.    
> I am scared to death that I will be incapable of loving my child, that I will look at them and only feel this crushing anxiety, and that I will ruin their life.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0277 -->
> ​I'm currently 9w5d pregnant after 2 losses last year (a chemical and a 12-week loss that happened literally a year ago yesterday). That loss happened spontaneously the morning of our 12-week scan, right when we were excited to hear the heartbeat and tell our family and friends.
> 
> ​Because of that, I am completely frozen waiting for my next appointment at 11+2. On paper, everything looks great. We’ve had 3 scans with our fertility clinic, baby is right on track, and betas were solid. But mentally? I’m in a total fog.
> 
> ​My husband and I run a small family business, and he works SO hard. He's way too busy right now to even worry about what I'm doing, and he's not holding anything over my head, but I feel immense guilt. I work remotely and I'm not pulling a quarter of my weight right now. I tell myself I "need" to sleep in, so I lie in bed until 7:45 AM while my husband gets up, takes care of the dogs, and leaves for work. I’ve always wanted to be the person who gets up early to move or go for a walk, but instead I just sit here on the couch for hours scrolling Reddit, beating myself up for doing nothing.
> 
> ​Physically, my pregnancy symptoms have actually been really mild, which almost makes the guilt worse. I had a tougher time around week 6, and Monday I felt pretty rough, but overall I felt more exhausted in the very beginning than I do right now. Because my symptoms aren't debilitating, I feel like I truly should be able to do more. Mentally though, I have capacity for literally nothing. I feel like I'm just finding excuses and "milking" the first trimester when I'm physically capable of pushing through.
> 
> ​On top of the anxiety and work guilt, I hate my body right now. ​Fitness was my core identity for 17 years. The gym was how I handled anxiety and felt good about myself. Over the past year, between processing the miscarriage and falling into a massive funk, I put on 12 lbs. I haven't even gained weight with this pregnancy yet, but starting out at my heaviest has me feeling like I don't recognize myself in the mirror. Before getting pregnant, I figured why bother losing it now if I'm just going to gain more anyway? But now that I'm here, I just hate how I look and I'm terrified of it getting worse.
> 
> ​People keep telling me to "give myself grace," but honestly? I feel like I've been giving myself "grace" for an entire year now, and I'm sick of it. I hate that my brain is hyper-fixating on my weight, my productivity, and my appearance when I feel like I should just be focusing on growing this little bub. But the mix of trauma, waiting for this milestone scan, and losing total control over my life and body is just overpowering right now.
<!-- /quote -->

### 9. Symptoms are judged by whether the parent can eat, sleep, work, travel or care for another child, not just by gestational week.

**Findings location:** §2 (symptoms), §§6–8 and §17.2. **Relationship:** Confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The broken week-by-week script includes persistent pain, exhaustion, vomiting and incontinence, repeated remedies and disrupted sleep.”

**What should change / remain:** Keep functional limits, failed remedies and the interaction of conditions visible. Severe nausea can defeat medication-taking, cooking and paid work; pain can make walking, lifting a toddler or driving impossible; diabetes demands can be demoralizing. Being told symptoms are normal does not answer the practical problem. Also keep specific relief and the wish for pleasure, rather than assuming that survival advice is all anyone wants. Individual remedies and proposed explanations remain their authors’ claims.

**Evidence:** 98 quote records assigned here for navigation; 30 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0033 -->
> I (33f) am a first time mom, 24 weeks pregnant with my rainbow baby and I feel like this pregnancy is ruining me mentally and physically. I'm super anxious for GD (testing in a few weeks) as well as preeclampsia. Everything birth terrifies me but we'll cross that bridge when I get to it. Things I am already doing:

<!-- quote:pbu18-0152 -->
> i'm 22w2d today FTM with a baby girl, and am considered high risk due to pregestational type one diabetes. i was ofc getting care from a regular OB but i've only recently started getting care from an MFM, and like honestly its kind of starting to feel like nothing i do will ever be good enough😅 my blood sugar control is supposed to be EXTREMELY tight and i mean i've been putting in as much effort as i can to get it within the range they want, my last a1c was a 6.1 which is just a hair above the optimal a1c they want. so far everything seems fine as far as ultrasounds and development, i'm getting a fetal echo ultrasound just to make sure. this is probably the first time in my 18 years of being type one diabetic that i actually resent it, like i have SO many risks and have to put in so much effort just to have a normal pregnancy. and like of course no matter what i'm gonna continue to put in as much effort as i can and i'm still SO excited to meet my girl, but idk it's getting a little demoralizing.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0153 -->
> Constant morning sickness all day - nauseous every second, dry heaving constantly. Only throwing up 2-3x a day now but it has been this way since 5 weeks. 
> 
> Yes, we talked to the OB already and have had Zofran on board since end of week 5. Went to the ER last weekend and ended up getting 2x bags of IV liquids & dextrose, as well as IV Zofran and Reglan. Felt better for about a day and was able to keep some food down for about 24 hours.
> 
> Aspirated some vomit and ended up with a respiratory infection I’m still fighting. Got a UTI from how dehydrated I was/still am. Incredibly constipated and bloated from taking the antiemetics.
> 
> Even with prescription Reglan, Zofran, Unisom/B6, ginger chews (can’t have them, the smell makes me puke), gum/mints, peppermint oil…I can’t do this. Already lost 10lbs in just over a week. I’ve had to call off work 4x now and can hardly move from the couch to the kitchen. 
> 
> Anything and everything is repulsive, and I was a big food lover before pregnancy - the farthest from being picky. The insanely heightened sense of smell has not done me any favors, either.
> 
> I have intense pelvic/hip/back pain, no matter where or how I try to sleep. I am exhausted but am constantly waking up in pain - even with the Unisom.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0154 -->
> Tomorrow I have to go back to work after being off for almost 10 days and I honestly don’t know how I will be able to get through the day. I am the only one bringing in income right now so I don’t have much of a choice, and won’t get paid sick leave without a note from my doctor.
> 
> My doctor put in an order for me to go to the nearest hospital (an hour away one way, we are very rural) 1-2x week for IV fluids/Zofran infusions. With the price of gas and me being the only one with consistent income, this feels so out of reach. 
> 
> I’ve been fighting endometriosis for 11+ years and we were so happy to finally get pregnant. Now, even after having episodes of passing out from endo-related periods/blood loss, pelvic pain, surgeries, etc, this is by far the MOST difficult time I’ve ever had in my life. It feels like an intense bout of the flu, a hangover, and mono all rolled up into one (+ not being able to poop, which is miserable in its own right). 
>

But the pain is horrible and I’ve been crying on and off all day cause of how much it hurts.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0233 -->
> **FAIL:** Low-dose iron supplementation during pregnancy.  
> ***why:*** My pregnancy iron levels were on the low to mid end of the spectrum, when they should have been mid to high. You never know how much blood you may end up losing. I was anaemic for a year after birth, after having two blood transfusions and an iron infusion at the hospital.  
> **FIX:** This time around (currently pregnant with second one) GP put me on high dose iron just in case. Talk to your GP about being sufficiently bolstered for birth, not just "adequate".
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0234 -->
> **FAIL:** PPIs, anti-acid diet.  
> ***why:*** Reflux became bad enough that PPIs and regular anti-acid advice didn't do anything.  
> **FIX:** Sodium alginate tablets, 30m after every meal, with no liquids or food for a while after. Note that some tablets have additional ingredients that should be consumed in limited amounts or not at all while pregnant, so check with your GP before using.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0235 -->
> **FAIL:** lactulose, psyllium husks  
> ***why:*** These didn't touch the third trimester constipation, and I was on a high fibre plant based diet with plenty of water.  
> **FIX:** Whole prunes, however many I needed to eat in order to get things moving. Toward the end it was about 8/day. My PP poop was completely ordinary, nothing scary or difficult about it.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0272 -->
> I’m 8 weeks pregnant (never been pregnant before) and I’ve been feeling absolutely miserable for the past 2 weeks.
> 
> I’m nauseated all day and throw up whenever I try to eat, so I can barely keep any food down. I can still drink fluids, but eating is really difficult. I’ve been prescribed Zofran, but it doesn’t seem to help with the nausea or vomiting.
>

>

> 
> The hardest part is how much this has affected me mentally. I’m so exhausted and sick all day that I catch myself feeling resentful toward this pregnancy, and then I immediately feel guilty for even thinking that. I don’t want to feel this way, but I honestly don’t know how much more I can take.
> 
> Yesterday I had what I think was a panic attack. I cried for about an hour because I felt overwhelmed, and it became difficult to breathe. I’ve contacted my OB’s office, and they basically told me to try taking my medication again and said these symptoms are normal in early pregnancy. But it’s now been 4 days where I’ve had almost nothing except water and a little bit of rice porridge. If I eat anything more than that, I throw up almost immediately.
>

### 10. Body changes can reactivate eating-disorder fears and shame, with partners and clinical paperwork adding to the distress.

**Findings location:** §6 (identity/body), §§2–3, 5 and 9. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Exercise appears as identity, a hoped-for preparation method, a source of guilt and a question about pain or safety.”

**What should change / remain:** Keep weight, hair loss, breasts, diastasis, appearance and intimacy in the parent’s own terms. “Give yourself grace” is explicitly rejected in some accounts; a blind weight can be disclosed inadvertently on paperwork. The products bought for appearance and the debate over paid hair/makeup are specific attempts to feel like oneself, not proof of demand for more generic reassurance.

**Evidence:** 8 quote records assigned here for navigation; 8 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0103 -->
> My husband just shattered me. I had just made up my lunch - a salad of iceberg lettuce, two chicken kebab skewers, some chopped up watermelon, a small piece of halloumi grilled cheese, a bit of steamed sweet potato, and a small bit of sourdough croutons. I was finished and went back to the kitchen to chop up the rest of the watermelon and put in a container for the fridge for my toddler. Then my husband asked me if I was getting more to eat. He said, “are you making another plate?” with a look of concern and a tone that said “put the fork down porky!”That was the moment I withered and died. 
> 
> I said, “what do you mean by that?” Knowing damn well wtf he meant. He knew his error and immediately tried to cover, then eventually responded to my repeated “what do you mean by that?” With- “I’m just trying to help you.”
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0104 -->
> I breastfed my first for 2 years and the weight was very slow to come off, and my Diastasis never really resolved. I’m now breastfeeding on demand. I am loosely tracking my food and getting in whatever exercise I can. Mostly it’s walking on a treadmill after meals, at least a mile a day, and doing some core workouts and body weight, strength training specific for postpartum diastasis recti healing. Sleep is hit or miss at this stage, four months is a common sleep regression, and she’s been a bit restless the last week or so. On top of it all I just came down with  a really terrible cold that has been horrible at night. I wake up with razor throat And cough my head off, waking her up.
> 
> Anyway, I just don’t even know what to do. I feel like I just lost all will to live. I’m certainly not suicidal, but I absolutely feel dead inside. I feel so unbelievably ashamed. I feel worthless as a mother. I feel like Jabba the Hutt. I feel heinous. The hair at my temples has all but gone. During pregnancy I grew out my natural medium brown hair, so where the hair fell there’s a stark contrast between my white scalp and the darkly sparse strands that are left. I bought some brown hair powder on Amazon, can’t wait to spackle it on. I’m turning 40 in three months, and we have a trip planned to Florida. I was so hopeful that I would not have this sagging apron belly and weird hernia above my belly button and round Pooch belly that looks about second trimester by the end of the day. I look at my face and I don’t recognize myself.  I have jowls, and my neck seems to be disappearing, becoming thicker and wider, as my face looks long and puffy. My breasts are monstrous, I’m wearing a 36H these days. They sag and they’re in the way of everything. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0111 -->
> So I've been working my ass to lose weight. I gained a lot of weight partly  because I had to get off my glp meds when I found out I was pregnant. When I say I gained a lot I mean like 70 lbs. I ride my stationary bike between 40-50 miles a week. I've been eating better and I even got back on my glp drug for almost 2 months now. I stopped pumping because it was taking a toll on my mental health and was physically struggling. 
> 
> I have a history with eating disorders. Had to be hospitalized and all that when I was younger. It's been a really long time since then and I've been doing well. Have struggled with my weight but haven't gone back to that. Before I got pregnant I finally felt like I got a handle on things and then I got pregnant...
> 
> I have avoided weighing myself and figured I would look when I felt I had made some progress. Told myself I would give it 2 months. Even when I went to the Drs office I would do a blind weight. ### 11. Repeated checking can briefly soothe, fail to soothe or create the next alarm; parents differ on how much reassurance they want.

**Findings location:** §6 (waiting and reassurance), §16.4 and §17.6. **Relationship:** Confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Fear and reassurance have a mechanism.”

**What should change / remain:** Name the loop: pregnancy-test lines, symptom scanning, movement, BP checks, early scans and the next milestone. The parent who recognizes a loop may still be unable to stop it. Distinguish the scan that enables planning from the scan that creates more uncertainty, and the request for positive-only stories from the request for both outcomes. Do not claim that more monitoring or less information always helps.

**Evidence:** 79 quote records assigned here for navigation; 20 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

### 12. Information can fail through interpretation, timing, recall or actionability even when the data and contact details are available.

**Findings location:** §§4–6, 10, 16.2 and 17.4. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “more information is not what she wants. She wants it sorted and fewer things in it”

**What should change / remain:** Add unreadable or unexplained scan reports, app dates that conflict with scans, results compared against a non-pregnancy reference range, and an alarming newborn chart interpreted before a clinician has explained it. AI can amplify fear, disagree on dates, read faint lines into tests or advise contacting a doctor without making the next step usable. A request for lived experience is sometimes explicitly neither reassurance nor advice; a moderator’s response can obstruct that request.

**Evidence:** 34 quote records assigned here for navigation; 22 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

**Further supporting IDs:** [pbu01-0379], [pbu01-0382], [pbu01-0383], [pbu01-0389], [pbu01-0459], [pbu01-0460], [pbu19-0140], [pbu19-0203], [pbu19-0211], [pbu19-0216], [pbu19-0219], [pbu19-0227].

### 13. Being told whom to call is insufficient when care is delayed, contradictory, dismissive or impossible to use.

**Findings location:** §5 (being believed), §§4, 6, 8–10 and §17.4. **Relationship:** Confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “the gap is information as much as contacts.”

**What should change / remain:** Include unanswered lines, weekend closures, rejected referrals, late disclosure of scan findings, unaffordable private alternatives, clinical consent after sexual trauma, and the emotional state that prevents recall or reaching an appointment. Parents may delay seeking help to avoid wasting resources or bothering staff. Keep prompt, kind and useful care beside these accounts, and distinguish an author’s claim of negligence from an independently established fact.

**Evidence:** 88 quote records assigned here for navigation; 24 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0015 -->

I genuinely don’t know how I’m supposed to sit in this unknown for weeks. I feel like I should’ve known about this sooner and I didn’t and now I’m feeling guilty. <!-- quote:pbu01-0133 -->
> The day quickly went down hill from here as I went to get my bloods. The woman went in a very small vein in my hand, which was painful and was filling the vial at a trickle. When she went for the second vial she dropped it, kicked it around with her foot, then picked it up and put it in. At this point I started feeling woozy and told her to stop. I started going blind and told her so and she left to get me some water. The next thing I knew I was on the floor, my phone and papers scattered, alarm blaring, surrounded by like 8 women. I was confused where I was. I was shaking and hyperventilating. They sat me up, cooled me down, watered me, fed me, propped my legs up, and took my vitals. After a minute I remembered where I was and that I must have fainted. The response was amazing! They were all so kind and they came running quickly. It was scary and embarrassing and dramatic. They want to try again later with me on a sofa but I’m thinking today’s not it 😅 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0090 -->
> I’m an immigrant who had her antenatal care until 21weeks. As I had a recent miscarriage (blighted ovum) I was really anxious and had multiple scans done due to cramping, my last scan being the 20w anomaly scan where I was said to be normal “low risk pregnancy” as per scan report.   
> As I felt more comfortable continuing pregnancy back home I flew back at 21w+6d and had an OBGyn visit just in case. I found that my internal os was open with my cervix measuring just above 5mm, my membranes were bulging. I had an emergency cerclage done with whatever cervix I had left and I’m on progesterone tablets and restricted mobility. 
>

>
