### 24. Pregnancy and postpartum can coincide with a parent or partner dying, changing the imagined family as well as the help available.

**Findings location:** New bereavement subsection in §§5–6 and 9. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Women with no village.”

**What should change / remain:** Add active grief, hospice caregiving, inability to attend a funeral because of recovery, and trying to share sonogram pictures and a heartbeat before a father dies. Missing a mother’s presence is also missing milestones, guidance and the relationship a child will never have. These accounts need more than arranging a visitor window.

**Evidence:** 9 quote records assigned here for navigation; 9 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0088 -->
> My father passed away suddenly a month ago. I’ve lost 14 lbs and I’ve hardly slept all month. I had been wanting to document and enjoy this pregnancy and instead I’m so miserable I only cry. I’ve been having mild cramps and back pain. And today my doctor wants to see me and do an ultrasound. I just have this overwhelming feeling I’m not bringing this baby home and I am not sure if that’s just because I’m so overwhelmed with grief or if it’s a sign something might happen.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0254 -->
> I had my baby by c section, a few days later, I ended up in the hospital with postpartum preeclampsia. My Dad who lives across the country fell and also was admitted to the hospital the same day. At 2 weeks postpartum, my dad passed away in the night. I didn't get to say goodbye. I also didn't get to travel to the funeral. I feel so heartbroken. It's been 3 months and I still have nightmares of his last days and feel so guilty and awful I was not there for him in the end and couldn't go to the funeral.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0347 -->
> Im so so torn because I don’t know if I can do a red eye at this stage, an almost 5 hour flight to boot, then another one, then a funeral. I want to just go home. BUT I also think I’ll regret missing this funeral for the rest of my life if I don’t go. For starters, it’s likely the last time I’ll ever see some of my cousins (not really what bothers me but it’s a consideration). It’ll absolutely gut my mom who has had to deal with all this alone because her siblings live out of province and she doesn’t have a partner. She begged me to come. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0308 -->
> I’m a FTM(20), about 6 months pregnant, and feeling a lil sad. My mother passed away a lil over a month ago. I don’t have many friends & a lot of the family I have are men. I’m feeling sad because I feel like im missing out on such milestones during pregnancy. The baby group im in on Facebook, all the women are chatting about their upcoming baby shower & some have already experienced it. All the lil things you do with your mother around this time, the pampering, special stuff. I’m not getting. I’m blessed with an amazing spouse & I do have a loving family, but I just feel very alone due to the lack of women & friends that are supposed to be around. 
> 
> I always imagined pregnancy with the gender reveal & baby shower, the celebration before & after. I know a lot of it is just the idea, but im sad it’s not happening at all. I just feel like im missing out on special things. 
> 
> I guess im ranting to ask if anyone else has gone through similar or has any words of encouragement in navigating this? I just wish I had a village & my baby + family was celebrated. 😕
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0346 -->
> I'm 31 from Aus. Some related background info: I have been pregnant 4 times in the past, each resulting in miscarriage. This was all with my last partner. After the 4th miscarriage (March/April 2025), I discovered my partner of 2 years had unalived himself. I'm not asking for sympathy but as you can imagine the loss of him and our baby absolutely broke me. It was completely unexpected and he always spoke openly about how excited he was to have kids and a future together. I had to get a D&C for the miscarriage and I went on to suffer post operative complications which prevented me from physically attending his funeral which made it even harder.
> 
> Since then I have been focusing on healing and have been actively engaging with support services. Grief is something that never really goes away, and I miss him everyday. I try my best to be grateful for what I do have. Don't get me wrong the first 6 months were very difficult and there are still some bad days, but I want to move forward with my life in whatever capacity that is and in a productive way. He would want me to be happy.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu19-0229 -->
> Anyways, I just wanted to vent. I have amazing friends / older figures in my life. I’m so grateful to have them! It just sucks having no maternal guidance or support from my parents + thinking about our daughter never meeting my dad 💔 I miss him so much. 😔
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu19-0252 -->
> I have my anatomy scan tomorrow and as long as everything goes well, I’m bringing the sonogram photos to my dad at the hospital and his doctors offered to use a Doppler on my belly while I’m there so he can hear the heartbeat while he looks at the pictures.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu19-0257 -->
> I also have gestational diabetes and noticed that distress has a huge impact on my glucose numbers so I feel like I’ve had to manage my grief (thankfully my numbers haven’t been too bad over the past couple weeks since he passed so it’s not as huge of a source of stress).
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu19-0283 -->
> I’ve been really trying to control my stress so that it doesn’t affect my baby girl. I haven’t been sleeping, my round ligament pain has been horrendous these last few days, I haven’t been able to fully rest because I’ve been at the hospital all day everyday, I’ve been helping set my dad up in hospice, doing my college work while next to my dad’s bedside, and listening to doctors come in with just back to back bad news, and worrying about losing my absolute best friend.
<!-- /quote -->

### 25. The job problem includes surviving symptoms and loss now, protecting disclosure, and paperwork that medicine cannot make exact.

**Findings location:** §8.6, §10 and §17.2. **Relationship:** Confirms and extends.

**Current conclusion:** “What spring work posts are about instead: surviving the job now.”

**What should change / remain:** Keep uncertain induction windows versus fixed due-date forms; another hospital visit just to obtain today’s note; illness that prevents earning or attending prescribed infusions; unsafe physical work; work after miscarriage; and disclosure associated with threatened workloads or a lost promotion. A calendar can organize questions but cannot resolve an employer’s refusal, a waiting list, an inaccessible treatment trip or sudden eviction. Policy and retaliation accounts remain the author’s understanding.

**Evidence:** 40 quote records assigned here for navigation; 17 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0001 -->
> The primary issue is, for medical reasons my doctors plan on inducing me between 36-38 weeks. Neither of these are my technical “due date” that is in all of my medical paperwork. To my job, it’s looking like I’m taking parental leave a month before my due date and I’m having trouble getting it approved. They say they need a new due date in some form of documentation from my obgyn in order to process my requested dates and that a three week window is proving hard to approve.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0002 -->
> I’m acknowledging trying to take leave a week early is a privilege. However, women in my family have a history of developing preeclampsia very fast and having to have emergency c-sections and I’m trying to create optimal conditions to avoid this. They did not have the luxury of having the opportunity to take time off early, I do and I’d like to take it. My mom almost passed away in birth with me and my older brother, so I’m really trying to give myself time to rest my body before delivery. Especially since I’m already showing signs of increased risks of developing preeclampsia.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0199 -->
> When I found out I was pregnant (2 weeks after second layoff), I was scared, but we made it work, unfortunately dipping into a lot of savings. The school I was teaching at was absolutely horrible though, and I didn’t feel safe teaching while pregnant. For reference, I had been assaulted by a student twice with no consequences for him, and had students throw chairs and other objects in the classroom, cuss me out, get in my face, etc. with no consequences. Also, I was the music teacher. I didn’t even have a room or a piano. Just me walking up and down stairs with a cart and my guitar to try to give the kids some artistic enrichment experience. It was HELL. Especially in my first trimester.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0200 -->
> Fast forward to March, my husband got laid off again in the first week. We weren’t able to make rent or car payments, and now that it’s April it’s gotten worse. Landlord just gave us a 3-day notice. We’ve tried to communicate with them to explain the situation and give us some grace, but no luck. My husband is trying his hardest to find a new job and I’m going to work every day to help as well. It’s just not enough. We’ve applied for all the programs and assistance we could, but it takes a long time. I just don’t understand why this is happening to us. I’m trying to hold it together but I’m crying every day. I don’t want to stress my babies out or go into early labor. I keep apologizing to them because I feel like I’ve failed them as a mom before they even got here. I’m so upset. We have nothing. We’re desperate.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0418 -->
> Now, I can’t just miss work. I work in manufacturing (super easy, non-strenuous job, but I AM on my feet all day). So I went in this morning and went to medical. They released me for the day but I had to go back to the hospital and get a note that says I was seen today. I was more than happy to get even more peace of mind so I was okay with that, even if it’s ridiculous that they wouldn’t accept the note the doctor wrote yesterday for today. And of course my OB is closed on Fridays. I’m still extremely anxious over the entire thing. I don’t want to see any blood after experiencing previous losses. I’m happy things are okay. 
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0477 -->

I am pregnant again now, and for the past 1,5 month I have been completely unable to work on my research.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0478 -->
> I haven't been fully honest about the situation at work, especially when everyone else seems to be moving forward so quickly and doing so much. This pregnancy is terrifying for me. My mental health has already been shaken by everything I have been through and I haven't fully recovered. On top of that, a few weeks ago I started experiencing pregnancy nausea. It is relatively mild, but I feel queasy all the time. And, as if that were not enough, I also got a horrible cold around the same time and it still hasn't gone away.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0040 -->
> The biggest issues are the fact that I don’t have a job, I have a very large amount of credit card debt, I am cross country from my support system, and the bills need to be paid. My boyfriend works full time and tries to help as much as he can but he makes less than $16/hr so it’s hard. I used to have a good job - I made $60k a year - but I left that job in the spring of 2025 due to a mental health breakdown. After leaving my job I started working independently and started a handywoman business. I was doing pretty good but I did take on debt during the start up phase and then I unexpectedly got pregnant and once the morning sickness hit (with a vengeance) I decided I could no longer do the physical work I had been doing up to that point.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0041 -->
> Some days I’ve even had very dark thoughts about not wanting to be here anymore. I try really hard to make it through the hard days. I reached out to my mom twice for help as she makes a lot of money but she is not helpful mostly I think because she is upset I live cross country from her. She also is nutty and can be selfish. So she suggested I do DoorDash to try to make money, I started this week. I have made some money, $300 in three days but I also put 300 miles on my car and went through $50 in gas, also I have pelvic girdle pain so I get really sore getting in and out of the car and walking around. It’s hard not to feel bitter towards her while I’m out there struggling to make ends meet.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0089 -->
> Hi, I am 15.5 weeks and this is my second child. I work in a  very high demanding target driven job. I have had nausea, back pain, headaches and generally feeling unwell since about 7 weeks. 
> 
> There's already 1 person on long term sickness in my team and I felt like I couldn't take more than 3 days off sick in the beginning of the pregnancy as I could see all my emails coming through and I was already picking up my colleagues work. However, I was given the chance to work more from home and generally just have to just tell my manager etc if I'm not feeling well - she's been good. 
> 
> However, I've struggled with the work and often have to sleep when at home during lunch and can tell I'm doing less work etc. but my manager was off last week so her manager took over and basically my team got a dressing down about activity we have been doing and to do more and it includes getting out and about more. It ended with me in tears and hr and the higher up finding out. 
> 
> I am more worried about how this is all affecting my baby, I had headaches that felt like I had to drill my head to get better and when the stress started my arm started tingling and getting pain from it, on top of my day to day symptoms. 
>

It's also worth noting that I am high risk, not sure if I should mention that?

>

<!-- quote:pbu18-0270 -->

When I got pregnant again this time I called my OB to ask for proactive support for HG but they just told me every pregnancy is different (aside: they also denied any additional monitoring despite my MMC because they said the baby stopped growing at ten weeks so they don't consider it a second trimester loss and therefore no change in what they do). It isn't for me. In fact I think it's gotten progressively worse. ### 26. Costs and coverage can change miscarriage treatment and whether essential care is reachable at all.

**Findings location:** §8, §10’s money limits and §§17.2/17.5. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The money stress is mostly bills, daycare and leave”

**What should change / remain:** Add the insured $6k D&C quote that changes treatment to medication, dependent-pregnancy exclusions, a gap between insurance plans, denial of special enrollment, threatened access to insulin, and the stated cost of abortion medication in weeks of allowance. These are immediate constraints; comparing a proposed guide with expensive care does not establish affordability or willingness to buy it.

**Evidence:** 6 quote records assigned here for navigation; 6 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

I have been to the urgent care and emergency rooms but no one really can tell me anything specifically about my pregnancy.   
> I don’t know how my baby is doing and I don’t entirely know how I’m doing.

### 27. Some buying questions have precise functional requirements and timing, not just product overload.

**Findings location:** §§4, 6 and 8.7. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Overload more than any single product”

**What should change / remain:** Retain the actual specification: a good but not expensive BP monitor, compatible Thule adapters, bras that will accommodate uncertain growth, office clothes useful after pregnancy, comfortable trousers with pockets, and entertainment that creates time to do chores. Requests and planned purchases differ from completed transactions; none sets a price for a Mamivio guide.

**Evidence:** 8 quote records assigned here for navigation; 8 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

### 28. Books, groups, doulas and apps are judged against a specific job, and some purported demand is a provider or founder speaking.

**Findings location:** §§4–6, 8, 10–11 and §17.4. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “Preparation sometimes backfires.”

**What should change / remain:** The detailed account of more than ten books failing is about profound mismatch with incapacity, not only a promised outcome or rigid plan. Local mother groups can disappear between pregnancy and birth. A postpartum doula question asks how much service to schedule. A UK buddy request seeks shared NHS context. Keep the returning doula’s pricing problem and the app founder’s promotional account labeled; neither is independent buyer evidence.

**Evidence:** 7 quote records assigned here for navigation; 7 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0081 -->

However now I am feeling called back to this work and am wondering where to even begin. I technically never received certification (you needed to have 3 births in a years time to receive it) but I did complete the coursework and reading. So I do feel confident supporting mothers and birthing parents I just don't know how important certification is for families considering hiring a doula. I have also thought about retaking the course just to say I have certification. I will of course continue my education in other regards such as lactation and bereavement support. I know the topic of certification importance is debated so I'm curious what others perspective is on it.

To ease myself back into birth work I've thought about offering my services as a mother's helper with a focus on the postpartum period and body work. > We were so happy. We started the whole routine of bloodwork, ultrasounds and OB appointments. But at the end of the first trimester we got hit with gestational diabetes. The fear came right back, along with that awful question of whether it was all going to happen again.
> 
> From there the appointments got more frequent and the ultrasounds went to every 2 weeks. She started insulin, checking her blood sugar 6 times a day, taking her blood pressure every day, tracking her weight, drinking 2.5 liters of water a day, plus a bunch of new meds like low dose aspirin, calcium and iron.
> 
> With all that going on it's really easy to lose track. Everything had to be written down and the whole history had to go to the OB. Paper wasn't realistic, so we tried a bunch of apps, and none of them covered everything. For a high risk pregnancy she would have needed 3 different apps, and she'd still be writing her blood pressure and blood sugar on a piece of paper to bring to the appointment.
> 
> That's when I decided to build an app just for her, with everything she needed to manage the gestational diabetes and the pregnancy as a whole. I go with her to every appointment and every scan, and I'm the one who remembers her meds, so I knew exactly what she was dealing with and what she actually needed. I kept adjusting it around her real routine.
> 
> The first version took me 2 weeks. Full of bugs, obviously, but she loved it right away. Everything was in one place, it was private, and no more carrying paper around. And it actually worked. Her OB could see the full history, figured out where the blood sugar control was going wrong, adjusted her insulin doses, and since week 16 everything has been under control.
<!-- /quote -->

### 29. Some voices are trying to conceive or reassessing fertility before there is a pregnancy to date or a birth to prepare for.

**Findings location:** §2’s trying/previous-pregnancy context, §9 and limits of §10. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “The candidate: a guide personalised by trimester and by pregnancy number (first / second / third+)”

**What should change / remain:** Retain infertility, fear of IVF, conception after loss, lubricant worries, testing routines and success after trying. The vaccine/exercise questions in this share are parents’ questions and retrospective associations, not evidence establishing a causal effect. Do not automatically assign these voices a trimester product or infer purchase intent.

**Evidence:** 8 quote records assigned here for navigation; 8 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

### 30. Historical routing also lost ordinary pregnancy questions and voices of relatives and young pregnant authors.

**Findings location:** §§9, 12–13 and method notes; some material fits no substantive section. **Relationship:** Extends.

**Current conclusion:** “No minors were quoted as target customers.”

**What should change / remain:** Separate an actual young speaker from a gestational-age phrase: “16+3” in one cat story is a pregnancy week, while a fifteen-year-old asks about her forty-seven-year-old mother. Other records explicitly describe a pregnant teenager. Preserve these voices without inventing age, diagnoses or customer status; the re-reading is research inclusion, not proof of a minor buyer segment.

**Evidence:** 3 quote records assigned here for navigation; 3 full fields below. These groupings are not mutually exclusive experiences or counts of people.

## Outliers and counter-examples

These qualify the patterns above. A positive outcome is not a guarantee, and a distressing history is not a prediction of repetition.

### O1. A difficult first postpartum period can be followed by connection and joy.

Qualifies any expectation of repetition in §§2, 9 and 17.4; the feeding choice and emotional outcome differ between pregnancies.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0021 -->
> When breastfeeding didn’t work out after a week, I stopped. We switched to formula and he’s thriving. That switch this early alone would’ve completely undone me the first time.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0022 -->
> I’m not saying this will be the case for everyone. But I do want to say this: A hard first postpartum experience does not automatically mean the next one will be the same.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu01-0162 -->
> I gave birth to my 3rd child 4 days ago now and besides the day 2 crash, I’ve overalls been in pure bliss. I suffered with the baby blues with my first, and definitely ppd with my second. This time, I’ve been smiling since my. Third has been born. I feel so complete.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu18-0109 -->
> I had my first daughter in June of 2022. Birth trauma, NICU stay, horrible PPD/A, OCD, and intrusive thoughts. It became so bad that 17 months PP I admitted myself into a mental health hospital for a week. 
> 
> We started trying for baby number 2 last February. Has a miscarriage and got pregnant again in October. I didn’t enjoy my first pregnancy, but I didn’t hate it either. This last time…BLISS. Except for being high risk, having to deliver 3 weeks early, and having a c section due to transverse lie, it was a wonderful experience. The c section wasn’t that bad. I’m able to breastfeed without supplementing this time and I love it so much. My baby sleeps so well compared to her older sister at this age. I’m not in a fog. I can actually breathe and see my baby as mine and not a stranger. 
> 
> I LOVE my oldest. She’s so funny and very smart. Her imagination brings me such joy. It took a long time to bond with her. I have few memories of her first year due to the antidepressant I had been on for 8 years. Very foggy.
> 
> My eyes feel so open this time and I haven’t felt depressed or anxious since before I got pregnant last October. I’m literally the happiest I’ve been in YEARS. 
> 
> My mom has told me that she feels I am replacing my oldest with my youngest. She asked what the difference was. Gee, I’m not in the deep darks this time. This whole experience has been better and healthier. It’s making me feel guilty. I absolutely miss time with just my oldest. It’s hard not giving her all my attention. 
> 
> I don’t want people to think I love my youngest more or that I’m favoring her. I feel I’m just trying to soak it all up and enjoy everything because I didn’t and couldn’t with my oldest. I’m also done having babies. 
<!-- /quote -->

### O2. Reassurance and peer stories sometimes work.

Qualifies the checking-loop pattern: scans, a heartbeat update and other people’s experiences can genuinely permit hope, planning or calm.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0261 -->
> I had a mmc last fall and have been having moments of anxiety during this pregnancy as a result of the trauma from that loss. It took several cycles for us to conceive again and felt so hopeless at times. I’ve held off shopping, planning the nursery or shower despite everything so far pointing to a healthy baby. 
> 
> Today we had our 20 week scan and baby is thriving, everything looks as it should, and baby is actually measuring two days ahead. 
> 
> I’m so happy. I have been trying to practice hope over anxiety and I think this milestone is just going to make that practice easier. I’m really on cloud 9 today and couldn’t be more excited for December to come. 
> 
> I know this isn’t everyone’s reality, but I hope someone out there reads this and sees there really can be hope after a loss.

### O5. A supportive village and treatment are not proof that postpartum distress will be absent.

Finances were secure. Everything was okay. It really was.

>

>

> 
> The thing is, when I look back, this isn’t the first time during pregnancy that I’ve noticed more aggressive impulses. ## What fits nowhere

These need new subsections or explicit methodological handling; the current pregnancy-guide structure does not provide an adequate home for them.

### N1. Pregnancy ending, or continuing without an anticipated baby-at-home route

The birth-guide structure has no adequate place for waiting for a pregnancy to end, the aftermath of loss, adoption, a termination that is chosen or uncertain, or a baby who may not come home. These require new treatment rather than being buried under birth fear. See [pbu01-0058], [pbu01-0059], [pbu01-0060], [pbu19-0111], [pbu19-0221] and [pbu01-0251].

### N2. Safeguarding, coercion and privacy around paternity

A visitor-boundary or partner-task chapter does not cover fear of child-protection involvement, compelled disclosure, custody threats, financial captivity, abortion pressure or explaining a pregnancy without disclosing rape. The service fears in [pbu19-0081], [pbu19-0082], [pbu19-0156] and [pbu19-0261], and the paternity-language request in [pbu01-0490], need their own place. These remain authors’ accounts rather than statements of legal policy.

### N3. Family structures and young speakers beyond the assumed couple

The two-mother IUI/loss account [pbu18-0068], the author who mentions two supportive partners, the teenager who reports a good scan, the pregnant teenager afraid of losing housing [pbu18-0173], and the fifteen-year-old worried about her mother [pbu18-0157] should remain visible. They do not establish a customer segment or authorize an inferred age.

<!-- quote:pbu19-0100 -->
> I feel like myself more now. I was content with our 3 kids. But I don't know if I can actually abort. I'm pro-choice, but after having two miscarriages before having our third baby, I never wanted that for myself. I keep finding myself dreading wiping because I'm afraid to find blood. I haven't yet but I've been cramping all day. I'm terrified to miscarry again, to lose yet another pregnancy. I'm terrified of pregnancy itself and just how bad my OCD gets during it. I'm terrified to make a mistake I'll regret. My two partners are amazing and support whatever conclusion I come to.
<!-- /quote -->

### N4. Gender disappointment, feared disability and inherited trauma

These are more specific than generic body-image or happy-family content. Keep the parent interpreting an unconfirmed newborn concern through previous special-education experience [pbu01-0174], [pbu01-0175], [pbu01-0176], [pbu01-0177]; fear of a daughter repeating sexual trauma [pbu18-0260], [pbu18-0264]; and the stated distress after a boy result [pbu19-0301]. Do not convert feared disability into a confirmed diagnosis or treat all gender distress as the same mechanism.

### N5. A father’s sexual intrusive-thought account and distress about pets

The explicit skin-to-skin/arousal and intrusive-thought passages [pbu01-0109], [pbu01-0110] have no developed section in the current findings; neither is an admission of an act against the child. The pet-rage discussion [pbu01-0164] and newborn/pet-management request [pbu01-0165] similarly concern a household problem beyond the existing partner/visitor plan.

### N6. Ordinary questions lost along with the material the old rule targeted

Keep the cat kneading a 16+3-week belly [pbu18-0001], the dog’s new affection [pbu01-0414], and small everyday problems. These demonstrate lost research breadth, not a prevalence claim.

<!-- quote:pbu19-0193 -->
> Just pulled up to the grocery store and of course there’s a huge red pick up truck in the maternity parking spot. There were two men inside and they were lighting their cigarettes or whatever. Then got out and went inside. It’s a two door so unless they’re hiding a pregnant mom in the backseat there was no one! They also had a bunch of ladders in the back so looks they were just coming from a job. Either way, how annoying and inconsiderate!!! There are so many other parking spots they could have chosen.
<!-- /quote -->

<!-- quote:pbu19-0250 -->
> I'm 17 weeks and all my clean clothes suddenly smell strongly like rotting mayonnaise! Makes me gag every time and I haven't changed anything in my routine. Is this just my pregnancy nose acting up or is this a sign something to pay attention to? Husband can't smell anything unusual of course haha.
<!-- /quote -->

### N7. Providers and founders speaking in a consumer-research corpus

The returning doula’s certification and pricing questions [pbu01-0081], [pbu01-0082] are supply-side evidence. The all-in-one app story [pbu18-0293] is a promotional founder account and cannot be counted as independent demand, a purchase, or proof that the app caused improved glucose control.

### N8. Active bereavement and care for a dying relative

The hoped-for grandparent, a partner’s suicide, hospice caregiving, and recovery that prevents attending a funeral need a bereavement account alongside the no-village account. The heartbeat/sonogram visit [pbu19-0252], grief and glucose experience [pbu19-0257], and D&C complications preventing funeral attendance [pbu18-0346] make the distinction concrete.

## Money

**180 quote records indexed.** This includes stated prices and amounts, actual and contemplated purchases, refusals or delays, gifts with stated value, booked/private services, compensation questions, coverage, debt, housing/property ties, income and ability-to-pay constraints. It does not turn a product-use mention into a completed paid purchase. No refund amount was found in this share. No amount is a valuation of the proposed Mamivio guide.

Where a full record already appears above, its financial wording is repeated here as exact, separately checked excerpts; otherwise the full record appears here. “Amount unstated” means the record supplies no purchase price, not that the service was free. General resource constraints remain distinguishable from transaction evidence in the words themselves.

### [pbu01-0278] — Job offer falls through and later interviews disclosed pregnancy before a formal offer.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0278 -->
> Alas, I quit without a plan, though, I had a job lined up through the government and where I live it takes several months to get within the bureaucracy.  I had the job, at the last minute, after waiting about 4 months, I was denied from the job (believed to be because of the pregnancy) but I was never given a formal explanation. Since I was denied from that job, I have applied to several, I mean every kind of job that you could think of though we’re talking at this point 24+ weeks pregnant: Private sectors, other government departments/agencies, etc. I have taken several interviews that have gone very very well, but in each interview I stupidly have relayed that I am pregnant, which now I realize is NOT a good decision to self admit prior to receiving a formal job offer. 
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu01-0279] — Savings are drained while dependence on partner creates guilt despite unpaid household work.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0279 -->
> I am now 29 weeks pregnant, jobless and starting to get a bit desperate or perhaps it’s depressed. My partner takes care of me and covers the cost of living expenses however I feel just awful because he also has other children himself he has to fiscally support and I don’t even have the baby of his born yet he’s paying for the cost of living for my son and I to be here which is rent, water and electricity. I manage to cover groceries, I furnished the home that we live in and take care of everything in the house, cooking, cleaning, laundry, etc.. I want to contribute, he’s never asked but I’m worried that it’s coming, I have so many bills stacking up at this point, I’ve absolutely drained my savings, I still have so much left to pay and I don’t know how I’m supposed to find a job that would hire me without feeling some type of way about me if I tell them I’m pregnant once I get hired and I’m like 30 some odd weeks pregnant. Luckily, I don’t show much that I am pregnant but it will come. Has anyone else mustered out of a position like this? Should I feel like a horrible person for not being able to contribute more than minimally?
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu01-0356] — Paid preconception travel becomes a request for testing after feared mosquito exposure.

<!-- quote:pbu01-0356 -->
> Hi all, first - be gentle with me. I feel like a Grade A idiot. My husband and I just went to Roaton, which is an island off the coast of Honduras. Booked and paid for before we knew we were pregnant (I’m 7 + 6 today). Well, we went last week, and of course I didn’t realize zika was even a concern until we were on the way. I covered up almost constantly, and used spray religiously. Like, in clothes on the beach religiously. However, I got bit 3 times when in my Airbnb. All 3 bites occurred at once and I didn’t see for a fact what bit me, but it seems like a mosquito bite. I’m  
> SICK with worry I somehow got zika. Cases are very low, but there’s always a risk. I have my first appt on Thursday, and I’m going to beg her to test me for my mental health. I am not doing ok. I need advice - how do I calm down? I see lots of women still travel to places where Zika has been and their OBs aren’t concerned, but I am really not handling this well. I’m not even sure what I’m asking - but solidarity or insight would be so appreciated.
<!-- /quote -->

I can’t even afford to eat right now much less make my car payments and rent and now I’m just freaking out that I won’t be able to take care of my child because I can’t even take care of myself. ### [pbu01-0435] — Researching parenting and supplies overwhelms despite money; fear of abandonment and access barriers complicate the decision.

> and thats the most difficult part, i know how painful an abortion is, but its also risking my current relationship to end even though i love him so much. i would consider him my best friend. but i feel like if i told him to stop the marriage and i want an abortion, we are over. we have talked about having a big family, but now that i have been doing the research on how to raise a baby/child and all the stuff you need to buy (which money isnt necessarily an issue) it feels so overwhelming and its not a responsibility i want, at least right now. he keeps telling me i would be a great mother but deep down i dont believe it. im scared of repeating the generational trauma in my family. and im scared (even though he keeps reassuring me) that he will leave me to take care of the child alone. thats another pattern with the women in my family and with my friends that are mom’s. i dont know if its my abandonment issues and complex trauma making me feel this way like “if i leave first im in control of how i get hurt” kind of way?? i dont know i feel so lost and scared and uncertain. i keep asking myself what i want but i really dont fucking know. ### [pbu18-0020] — Family housing plan replaces reliance on an inconsistent former partner.

> ​He has completely changed. He is not the same person I knew, and the cold way he talks to me now hurts so much. I guess... I am just so  sad that this is where we've ended up.
> 
> I am planning to file a claim in the small claims court for the money he owes me (aside from child support). I have evidence of him agreeing to pay me back.

I’m diagnosed with bpd and my medication works well normally but all I can think about is work. I’ve not been there long. About a month but I’m just dreading it. I work part time aswell. All I can think about is quitting. I’ve even had thoughts about how I’d rather be in hospital than at work. I’m just constantly drained. The exhaustion is something I didn’t think was Possible at this stage. I’m just after some advice if others have gone through this, I know financially I’d be ok but it’s more what

### [pbu18-0028] — Constipation pain leads to emergency assessment and several attempted remedies.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0028 -->
> Im currently 5w5d and I’m already having the worst constipation of my life. I was cramping so badly all day Saturday that I ended up in the ER because my OB was worried it might be ectopic. They haven’t completely ruled that out because doctors couldn’t see anything on an ultrasound, but I’m beginning to think it was caused my gastrointestinal issues. I am *beyond* bloated and my belly is rock hard and I am at a loss of what to do. After a bit of miralax, I got some relief, but I’m still in pain. I’ve ordered Magnesium Citrate and Benefiber to start taking daily, but man, this sucks!!
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0029] — Previous losses are unknown to relatives who respond coolly and impose buying rules.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0029 -->
> I’ve had a chemical pregnancy and a miscarriage which they don’t know about and at one point I wasn’t even certain my body would let me keep a pregnancy. We are visiting their country next week and we wanted to let them know of the pregnancy before since my body definitely looks fat and bloated and pregnant. We also wanted to tell a few other family members but wanted to tell them first. 
> 
> When we called them albeit it was was late in their country, it was the most lukewarm reaction and I was taken aback considering how much they always brought up us having kids. 
> 
> Then my FIL calls my husband back and tells me how I have to eat healthy which I’m aware and I’ve been eating well for the baby. He also shares we can’t buy ANYTHING for the baby until they are born as per their countries superstition. 
> 
> I was so excited to see them and tell them but now I’m almost embarrassed to see them with how deadpan their reaction was. 
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0045] — A positive test after apparent menstruation prompts concern about ectopic pregnancy.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0045 -->
> Been TTC since the beginning of the year. It’s definitely been a struggle the second time around so last month I decided to try fertility acupuncture. Did it consecutively for three weeks and then my period was supposed to come Saturday, September 19, but it was late by three days. I took pregnancy tests leading up to it and they were all negative and lo and behold my period comes on Tuesday the 22nd. It lasted for five days, which was normal, but wasn’t as heavy and I had way less clots. Which I figured the acupuncture is benefitting me .
> 
> Well fast-forward to today & I’ve been very lightly spotting nothing crazy but when I wipe there’s some light pink blood.
> 
> Decided to take a pregnancy test today and it was a faint positive 🤯 
> 
> Very worried that this could be a possible ectopic & am planning to see my doctor ASAP. 
> 
> But has anyone had what they thought was their period and turned out to have a successful pregnancy?
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0055] — Friend seeks a way to acknowledge motherhood after miscarriage.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0055 -->
> I have a friend from high school that I hadn’t seen/heard from in 10 years. She moved to the same state as me recently and we would chat occasionally. One day she messaged me saying she was pregnant and wanted to tell me because I’m the only person she knows from our class that has children. A few weeks later she confided in me that she miscarried in a very traumatic way. 
> 
> She’s been very open about her miscarriage on social media, it seems like it’s therapeutic for her to talk about her baby.
> 
> Something I said to her that she seemed to find comfort in was that her motherhood is real, no matter how brief it may have felt. I want to have flowers delivered for Mother’s Day, what would be an appropriate note to write along with them? Would “happy Mother’s Day” seem insensitive?
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0057] — Conflicting tests recall prior miscarriage and prompt contact with an OB.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0057 -->
> I always test several times. I tested 3x yesterday and one was positive, two negative. Tested 2x today and one of each. I'll grab more tests soon, but has anyone else had this happen?
> 
> The only time I've had it previously was when miscarrying, but I don't have any of my usual miscarriage symptoms. Just feel tired as all hell. 
> 
> I've left a message for my OB so hopefully she'll call me back soon.
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0058] — Unexpected pregnancy threatens childcare access after recovery from postpartum depression.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0058 -->
> We have a little boy who has just recently been accepted into a state daycare after a long waiting list. We were genuinely so happy about it — he would have started in January. When we visited, he absolutely loved it. He’s very social, curious, and thrives around other children and new experiences.
> 
> Now I keep thinking about what this might mean for him… whether he would lose something important to him if everything changes. And it honestly hurts more than I expected.
> 
> At the same time, we don’t really have any support system around us. Financially, private daycare isn’t an option, and I can’t really imagine managing two small children at home alone most days. That part feels overwhelming and a bit scary if I’m being honest.
> 
> And yet… there is also a new life here.
> 
> We’ve always wanted a sibling for him. It just doesn’t feel like I expected it to feel. I’ve only recently started to feel more stable again after a difficult postpartum period with depression and insomnia, and it feels like we’ve only just begun to find our rhythm as a family.
> 
> There’s also the practical side — money, energy, emotional capacity. I’m not sure how much of myself I still have to give right now, and that thought makes me feel guilty.
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0060] — Prior absent bonding creates fear of both recurrence and unequal feelings for another baby.

Full record: Pattern 21. Financial wording:

<!-- excerpt:pbu18-0060 -->
> I felt completely disconnected from the pregnancy thinking how am I going to do everything and pay for 3 people all by myself, I wasn’t excited I was terrified, I remember thinking when she’s born and she’s on my chest I’ll feel something, I’ll feel that over whelming love a mother feels as soon as her baby is in her arms and when to came to it, I didn’t.
<!-- /excerpt -->

<!-- excerpt:pbu18-0060 -->
> I thought she was cute but she didn’t feel like she was mine, a month after she was born I found out her father was cheating and it completely broke me, after everything I done, I obviously left him and had very little to do with her and eventually stopped seeing her completely, I had a pretty bad break down and was diagnosed with severe postpartum depression, obviously now I love my daughter more than anything thing in the world, I would die for her but I can’t even look at baby photos of her without crying thinking to myself why didn’t I love you the way a mother was ment to love her baby, my fiancé tells me everything will be different this time, and he’s here for me every step of the way and I’m not alone doing it this time, but the two biggest things in my mind is what if this time I do feel the love and then have the guilt eating at me about how I felt about my daughter or what if I don’t feel anything again and hate myself for it…and it’s the cycle of feeling like there’s something wrong with me, don’t get me wrong I do want another baby but I feel torn on the fact either way I’m going to be mad at myself…has anyone else gone through something like this and can maybe offer me some advice or  tell me what happened when it came to having another baby
<!-- /excerpt -->

### [pbu18-0095] — A private opinion conflicts with serial hospital scans and repeated worry.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0095 -->
> That time I got angry and I went privately to a proper doctor, that was 9 october. The doctor there told me it is too early to be measuring anything because it’s just too small to have a reliable reading from any measurements and there is no point reading any heartbeat at this time either, he done a scan for me and checked everything he said the pregnancy is in a good place and there is the flickering of the heart which is most important and he advised me to not go for the scan on 15th because they are only stressing me and that I should just wait for my 12th week scan at this point. But i went today anyway and same thing happened. Slow heartbeat according to them, they measured the baby to 5.5mm CRL. I got really angry and started discussing so they referred me for a second opinion with a proper doctor from maternity unit instead of constant nurses consultations. I just don’t understand whats going on anymore, what to expect. I dont have any bad symptoms, no cramps no bleeding or spotting, I have normal pregnancy symptoms like nausea and breast pain and tiredness, I am really stressed because of it all lately. I just wish someone maybe had similar experience and could tell me their stories, even if it didnt end well.
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0096] — Eight weeks postpartum, family housing offers an exit from recurring partner deception.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0096 -->
> I really thought things would be different after the baby arrived. He really is a great dad and loves our daughter who is 8 weeks old now. But he lied to me again two weeks after coming home from the hospital. And he is the person who will keep lying and lying until I present him with evidence. I feel like therapy is just him trying to find an excuse. I feel that there's no deeper meaning other than he lied and got caught. Repeatedly. 
> 
> My sister has said that me and the baby can stay with her for a few months so that I can decide if I want to stay with him or get divorced since I am on maternity leave until the end of July. I just don't trust him anymore. When the bigger issues come, I know that he will lie. Nowadays if I ask him a question, I immediately ask myself why I even bothered asking because I don't believe his answer. I told him yesterday that I'm going to stay with my sister and he can see the baby on his two days off of work each week. Of course, he's upset. But I believe it's only sadness for the baby. I don't think he's really gave a crap about me at all to have treated me this way. 
> 
> Is this reasonable of me to do? I doubt myself because I know if the tables were turned, I'd be heartbroken if I couldn't see my baby as much anymore. But I can't stand living with him right now. Every day, I sit here getting more and more upset about our situation and I just totally resent him. I'm mad that he has destroyed our marriage. I'm mad that if we get divorced, he will have his family's inheritance & house to lean on, but I will probably have to move out of state as I cannot afford to live here on my own (we live in southern California). 
<!-- /quote -->

I just want to do the right thing. For myself for the baby and everyone involved... Should I have an abortion, given the crazy extenuating circumstances involving a non resident of Canada. ### [pbu18-0171] — Partners disagree under a short abortion deadline despite potential family support.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0171 -->
> I've known my boyfriend for 20 years, since we were teenagers. I'm now 33 and he's 35. We've been dating for about 2 years now and we found out I was pregnant 5 days ago. He's spiraling. He doesn't want to be a dad, he doesn't know how. He doesn't know where we'd live or how we'd support it. He didn't have a great example of how to be a dad from his father and his family doesn't live close by to us. His mom also passed away a few years ago. I come from a huge family and they are all nearby and would help and be supportive and I just feel like even though it's not ideal, I can do it and everything will be okay. He says it's gonna  ruin our lives and make us hate each other. It's very difficult because after 20 years he's one of my best friends besides being my boyfriend and I know that in his mind it's not logical to have a baby but I can't just ignore my own feelings for him. We don't live together, I still live with my parents because I've honestly never felt the need to move out but he has a roommate. We do live in an expensive city but he makes pretty decent money and I make less but honestly my parents pitch in and help me a lot. I'm only 5 weeks pregnant and the abortion time limit is 6 weeks here. I told him I didn't need him to be a dad if he doesn't want to be but he said he's not gonna pretend it's not his child for the rest of his life. 
<!-- /quote -->

### [pbu18-0345] — New blood-pressure monitoring leads to possible early delivery and concern about neonatal health.

<!-- quote:pbu18-0345 -->
> 35+5 today. Yesterday took my blood pressure at Walmart, it was 144/95, which is high in general, not crazy high but high for me. My last appt it was 116. So anyways I bought and at home monitor and was keeping an eye on it, it was trending high- anywhere between 135-155. Called dr and they said to go in the L&D. 
> 
> They did NST- baby is perfect. My blood pressure was going up and down but was not alarming (lowest 122, highest 148). They did blood work and pee test. Dr came back and said my platelet level is low and right at the cusp of preeclampsia, I believe he said 100,0001. Preeclampsia is 100,000. I have no other symptoms expect this blood pressure spike and the platelets. He said they would likely delivery at 37 weeks of my levels keep trending down. If before 37 weeks and it becomes urgent I would likely need C section. 
> 
> They gave me a steroid to help accelerate her lung development just in case she needs to be delivered. 
> 
> Has anybody been through this? I'm so nervous about her coming this early and having any health issues. 
<!-- /quote -->

## Requests

**126 quote records indexed.** These are concrete product/resource asks, requests or attempts to obtain tests/treatment/support services, and requests for a practical plan or routine. They include partners and relatives where identified by the source. A request about a medicine, test, abortion service or care pathway is not a request to buy an information product. General pleas for reassurance or shared experiences are retained in the patterns rather than counted as guide demand.

Each entry identifies the request through the source record’s short context. If its full wording is already reproduced, the entry points to it; otherwise its entire quote field follows. Explicit requests to hear only positive outcomes, both outcomes, or experience rather than advice are especially clear in [pbu01-0011], [pbu01-0037], [pbu01-0329], [pbu19-0152] and [pbu19-0280].

<!-- quote:pbu18-0364 -->
> Sorry not sure if this question even makes any sense…   
> So backstory last month my husband and I decided to  
> Skip trying last month because I needed to get some tests done and a procedure that required sedation. I didn’t want to possibly pregnant because I had a miscarriage over the summer and didn’t want to risk anything going “wrong”. But basically we had sex one time 7 days before I had an LH surge on my fertility tracker. Then we waiting 5 days after the surge before we had sex again. I’ve never really done the “calendar” method before and clearly it didn’t work because I ended up getting pregnant this cycle.   
> I’m just concerned because one I had X-rays and was under anesthesia all while being early pregnant and on top of that it just doesn’t make sense as we had sex several days before my surge so that means the sperm that was in there was likely several days old and I thought it can only live for 5 days in the reproductive tract. So basically I’m just concerned about the progression of this pregnancy given the circumstances. Did any one else get pregnant with a healthy pregnancy where they had sex days before their so called “fertile days” and peak fertility. I’m just genuinely confused. And for the record my periods are very predictable every 28 days and I always ovulate CD 16 or 17. Thanks not sure if this even even the best community for this post but if you have any other suggestions where to  
> Share please let me know.

## Coverage and validation

All three files were read from first record to last: ur01 502/502, ur18 363/363, ur19 334/334. Every record has a primary pattern assignment and is either reproduced or named as further support. The ledger additions were used as leads, then checked against the actual quote fields; counts and impressions from the older exclusion process were not adopted as prevalence.

**Programmatic check:** 735 full quote blocks match their record’s entire `quote` field exactly; 261 financial excerpt blocks occur exactly within the corresponding field. All 1,199 mentioned source IDs exist in this share. Every quoted current-conclusion fragment was also checked as an exact substring of the current findings. All schema-tagged purchase/price records are included in the Money index.

**Coverage:** quotes in the share: 1199; quotes reproduced verbatim: 709 distinct records (entire quote fields; repeated blocks counted once); further IDs listed without a verbatim field: 490; IDs not mentioned at all: 0.
